    RN-711 — The conversational agent knows where it is in the flow, and the user's technical restriction becomes a decision_record

    • Rule: the seven conversational agents (Creative, PO, Architect, Infra, Dev Lead, UX Designer, Staff) receive on every LLM call an EPHEMERAL system message with the delivery flow — the seven stages in order (Creative → PO → Architect → Infra → Dev Lead → dev agents → QA/SecOps → manual merge), WHERE the agent is, the real NEXT step and WHO triggers it (human × system) —, through the EngineApiClient facade, after the environment (RN-706) and before the profile (RN-680) and the language (RN-622, always the last). The source is ONE, Engine.Harness.FluxoDoTime, checked by a test against packages/contracts/fluxo.yml: every role cited exists there as active, and the text does not cite a stage outside it (deploy, pipeline, Dockerfile, InfraAgent). The Creative records the user's EXPLICIT technical restriction (language, "no dependency", "no network", "a single module") as a decision_record (emit_artifact, RN-505), without a lecture; the Architect's kickoff reads the session's artifact.decision_record (the same read by type, RN-580) and says to respect them, stating WHICH and WHY when going against one. The Architect's wrap-up does not ask again for a confirmation already given, and the Infra does not paste the files into the chat nor promise deploy.

    • Measurement (AT-369/AT-370, 10/02, loja-teste): the Creative announced the Architect as next (it is the PO); the PO said the Architect would "implement"; the Architect, that "dev agents start" after the ADRs, and asked "Confirme: Arquitetura pronta" after the confirmation; the Infra dumped ~80 lines of compose into the thread. The request "Node puro, nenhuma dependência, sem rede, um módulo só" did not become an artifact and the Architect created three modules.

    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/harness/fluxo_do_time.ex:107 (texto), :97 (anexar), apps/engine/lib/engine/sessions/engine_api_client.ex:647

      :97 (anexar), apps/engine/lib/engine/sessions/engine_api_client.ex:730 (llm_turn), apps/engine/lib/engine/harness/agents.ex:23 (identity), apps/engine/lib/engine/agents/arquiteto_server.ex:404 (build_kickoff)

    RN-714 — SAST that did not run leaves the SecOps gate pending, never approved

    • Rule: when the SecOps gate's semgrep does not run — missing binary, invalid output (:invalid_output), :sem_saida, exception or time ceiling —, no verdict is recorded: the PR stays in awaiting_secops, the gate stays PENDING and blocking, and the PR does not reach the user as approved by SecOps. The reason goes to the thread as an agent.error of origin infra ("SAST não rodou: — … reexecute o gate depois de corrigir"). The external contract does not change: the usual two verdicts remain (approved/changes_requested), one per gate. A missing gitleaks is still only "skipped" in the summary.
    • Re-run: the cycle stays in_progress in gate_states, and the GateRescuer re-runs it at engine boot and after the staleness threshold (gate_rescue_stale_after_seconds, 15 min). There is no re-run gate button on screen.
    • No repetition (AT-386): a rescue that finds the SAME reason again does not record another agent.error: if the SecOps's last event for that task (agent.error or artifact.secops_verdict, read by type, tail with a ceiling) is already this error with the same reason, only the server's Logger line remains. A different reason, a verdict in between or a failing read record again; the agent.error now carries taskId.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/secops_agent_server.ex:116 (run_secops), apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/secops_agent_server.ex:144 (sast_nao_rodou), apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/secops_agent_server.ex:209 (erro_ja_registrado?)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/gates/secops_agent_server_test.exs ("sem achados (gitleaks e semgrep limpos): approved"; failure cases: "semgrep com saída inválida: gate pendente, sem veredito, motivo nomeado" and "semgrep com binário ausente: gate pendente, sem veredito, motivo nomeado"; AT-386: "primeiro resgate grava; o segundo, com o mesmo motivo, não", "motivo diferente, ou veredito depois do erro, grava de novo", "o SAST que volta a rodar aprova como sempre")
    • Revises: RN-707 ("o veredito do gate NÃO muda")
    • Origin: AT-380

    RN-717 — The agent deviation the Anamnese observes becomes a product signal, without personal data

    • Rule: an agent loop, a turn cancelled by the user and a user message that repairs an agent failure are recorded by the Anamnese as anamnese.agent_deviation (tool report_agent_deviation), never as the person's profile. The payload is assembled in the engine by an allowlist — agente, tipo (laco | cancelamento_pelo_usuario | reparo_pelo_usuario) and evidenceEventIds (deduplicated, ceiling of 20) —, without free text and without the user's id, name, e-mail or trait; an extra field is discarded. The type is not projected into the user's graph. A type outside the vocabulary or without evidence is refused, without recording.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/anamnese/tools/report_agent_deviation.ex:73 (payload), apps/engine/lib/engine/anamnese/tools/report_agent_deviation.ex:52 (run), apps/engine/lib/engine/anamnese/tools.ex, rule in the kickoff (prompts/anamnese-kickoff.md v4)

    One cache key, one question (RN-718)

    RN-719 — An agent tool accepts a list sent as JSON text, and an exception inside it goes back to the loop

    Context: measured live (10/02, Haiku 4.5 via OpenRouter), the model sends lists and objects as a JSON STRING ("business_rule_ids": "[\"01M…\"]"). Three outcomes: create_story silently discarded the ids (the story stayed draft), other tools refused and cost rounds, and create_c4_diagram did Enum.map on a string and brought down the Architect's turn with agent.error.

    The rule: every tool of the seven conversational agents and of the ToolLoop runs through ONE point, Engine.Harness.ArgumentosDeFerramenta.executar/4. An argument whose schema declares array/object and arrives as a valid JSON string of the type becomes the value; a string that is not is a NAMED refusal ("o campo X precisa ser uma lista"), before the tool runs. A field declared string is never touched. An exception raised inside the tool becomes tool.result ok: false with origem: "codigo", and the error enters the loop like any other (RN-163): the turn continues.

    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/harness/argumentos_de_ferramenta.ex:26 (executar), :73 (coagir), :56 (falha_interna?); apps/engine/lib/engine/agents/resultado_de_ferramenta.ex:29 (payload); apps/engine/lib/engine/harness/tool_loop.ex:409 (run_direct); apps/engine/lib/engine/agents/arquiteto_server.ex:357 (executar_ferramenta, the same helper in the other six servers)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/harness/argumentos_de_ferramenta_test.exs (create_story with ids in a JSON string records the ids; create_c4_diagram with actors in a JSON string works; a non-JSON string in an array field is a named refusal; a tool that throws does not end the ToolLoop turn and records tool.result ok: false)
    • Origin: AT-387

    RN-721 — A shortened story or task id is resolved within the project

    The Architect passed to assign_story_modules the first 8 characters of the story's id, the api responded 400 "storyId must be a UUID" and, after eight attempts, no story got a module (loja-teste-2, 10/02). Every agent tool that receives a story or task id — assign_story_modules and create_task (story_id), assess_implementability (storyId) and propose_execution_plan (tarefas[].taskId) — passes the id through Engine.Harness.IdDoBacklog before calling the api:

    • A full UUID goes on without a query.

    • A prefix of at least 8 hexadecimal characters is resolved against the PROJECT backlog (list_backlog): a single id starting with it becomes the UUID; none is a named refusal ("nenhuma história do projeto tem id começando com …"); more than one is a named refusal ("o prefixo … é ambíguo"). The refusal goes back to the model as the tool result (RN-163).

    • What is neither a UUID nor a prefix goes on as it came, and the api refuses it.

    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/harness/id_do_backlog.ex:21 (resolver), :43 (resolver_em); apps/engine/lib/engine/agents/dev_lead_tools.ex:180 (resolver_tarefas)

    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/harness/id_do_backlog_test.exs (a unique prefix resolves; ambiguous and nonexistent refuse by name)

    The Anamnese does not pay twice for the same window, and the gate rescue does not restart a cycle that is waiting for someone (RN-722)

    RN-722 — The Anamnese closes the window it evaluated and only runs with new interaction; the gate rescue asks about the task, respects the pending human decision and parks the old cycle

    • Rule (Anamnese): (1) the round that EVALUATED the window and ended with no profile (skip_proficiency) writes anamnese_runs with zero profiles, and the next one starts at window_to — this revises the clause of RN-680/of the moduledoc "a round that does not conclude does not write": a round that FAILS (ceiling, budget, provider) still does not write, and one that never reaches the LLM (no subject, triage, daily ceiling) closes nothing; (2) the events the Anamnese itself writes (anamnese.* and every event of actor agent/anamnese) and those of actor system stay out of the window and of the triage count; (3) the owner's decision of 02/10: a subject is whoever has at least 5 NEW interactions of their own (events of actor_kind: "user" plus decisions, in the window that now starts at the end of the last attempt) — this revises the "at least ONE" of RN-680 —, and the project has at most 4 paid rounds per UTC day (concluded, failed or run_skipped written by the model; a skip with no subject does not count). Below either of the two, no call to the LLM or to the RAG; the reason goes to the log and, only for a hand-requested round, to anamnese.run_skipped (causa: "teto_diario" or "sem_sujeito_elegivel").
    • Rule (gate rescue, revises RN-140): the GateRescuer (a) considers alive the cycle whose TASK is in flight in the lead, by asking it ({:em_voo?, task_id}), and no longer the project process — an idle lead does not hold back the rescue; a lead too busy to answer counts as alive; (b) does not restart a cycle whose session has a PENDING proposed_action from a gate actor (qa*, secops*, appsec) — it only records it in the log; (c) the owner's decision of 02/10: a cycle stopped for more than 2 h since the LAST ACTIVITY (updated_at) is PARKED (parked_at) with gate.rescue_parked in the session log (task, gate, age, reason), is resumed neither at boot nor on the tick, and resuming requires a human gesture (GateRescuer.retomar_estacionado/3).
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/workers/anamnese_worker.ex:189 (fechar_janela), apps/engine/lib/engine/workers/anamnese_worker.ex:104 (dentro_do_teto_diario?), apps/engine/lib/engine/session_events/event.ex:194 (project_window_query), apps/engine/lib/engine/session_events/event.ex:202 (count_anamnese_rounds_since), apps/engine/lib/engine/anamnese/elegibilidade.ex:67 (min_interacoes_proprias), apps/api/src/application/use-cases/anamnese/record-proficiency.use-case.ts:160 (fecharJanelaSemPerfil), apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/gate_rescuer.ex:379 (task_em_voo?), apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/gate_rescuer.ex:403 (aguardando_humano?), apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/gate_rescuer.ex:321 (estacionar), apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/gate_rescuer.ex:359 (retomar_estacionado), apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/qa_lead_server.ex:95 (handle_call)

    • Origin: AT-391 (real use of 02/10: ~US$ 8.90 of Anamnese in a day with nobody asking; a gate cycle restarted with a pending action and another 8 h with no rescue), the owner's decisions of 02/10

    Revised by RN-923 (ADR 0226). The AUTOMATIC rescue (tick) of a cycle holds once: the second parks with motivo: "retomada_automatica_ja_usada" and waits for the "Resume gate", which resets the counter. The 2 h parking stands as is.

    RN-723 — The Infra inherits the network the Architect declared for the project

    Owner decision on 10/02 (ADR 0208), revising the literal none network of the start on acceptance of RN-671. The Infra's election uses the network of the most recent artifact.project_image emitted by the Architect (actor_id: "arquiteto", any session of the project), read locally by the engine; without a decision of his, or with a value outside none/egress, it stays none. It applies to the server's start on acceptance and to the propose_container_start tool without network. The seeded autonomy does not change: egress starts without a click, and the price (the egress network reaches the machine's published ports, ADR 0189) is declared in the ADR.

    The Anamnese does not pay twice for the same window, and the gate rescue does not restart a cycle that is waiting for someone (RN-722)

    RN-724 — The parked gate cycle comes back through a button in the thread, not only through the operator's terminal

    • Rule: the gate cycle PARKED by the rescue (RN-722, ADR 0207) is resumed through POST /projects/:projectId/tasks/:taskId/gates/:gate/resume, with the SAME minimum role as whoever decides the PR (developer). The api only authorizes and asks the engine (POST /internal/projects/:id/tasks/:taskId/gates/:gate/resume), which delegates to GateRescuer.retomar_estacionado/3: a cycle that does not exist, is not parked or a gate outside qa/secops is 409 gate_nao_estacionado and nothing is dispatched. In the session thread, the gate.rescue_parked event becomes a notice with the "Retomar gate" button, inert with the reason in text when the role does not reach developer; the refusal shows the api's sentence. No new poll: the resumed gate's progress arrives through the session log.

    • Origin: AT-397, closing the gap declared in RN-722

    RN-725 — Text with a literal escape is decoded, and the argument refusal says what arrived

    Context: in TP-01 (10/03, Claude Haiku 4.5) the PO recorded story titles with the LITERAL unicode escape (código): the normalization of RN-719 only decoded array/object fields. Recreated with the right title, the stories went through the duplicate refusal (RN-720/RN-081), which did not match the escaped one with the normal one. And the "exige X" refusals did not say what had arrived: the PO spent 28+ steps (48% of the test's cost) up to the ceiling, and complete_story replied "ainda faltam RF, DoD, DoR ou business_rule_ids" without saying which.

    The rule:

    1. At the same point as RN-719, a string with a LITERAL \uXXXX (surrogate pair included) is decoded, and with it \n, \t, \" and \\ of the SAME string. A string without \uXXXX comes back byte for byte — code in a content with a literal "\n" does not change. This revises RN-719's "a string field is never touched" only in this case.
    2. The EXACT duplicate of a story (api) and of a rule (engine) compares the title with the escape decoded, before the usual case folding and NFD.
    3. A tool refusal that says "exige X" gains, at the same point, the KEYS that arrived (names, never values), or that a string/list arrived instead of an object. emit_artifact with a key missing in the payload states the missing keys and the received ones, instead of {:missing_keys, …}.
    4. complete_story names exactly what is missing (falta: business_rule_ids), by the same yardstick as the api's missingForReady, read from the returned story.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/harness/argumentos_de_ferramenta.ex:102 (decodificar_escapes), :152 (chaves_recebidas), :166 (com_chaves_recebidas); apps/engine/lib/engine/harness/tools/complete_story.ex:80 (faltam); apps/api/src/domain/backlog/story-overlap.ts:39 (decodificarEscapesLiterais), :25 (normalizarTitulo); apps/engine/lib/engine/harness/artifact_dedupe.ex (normalizar)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/harness/argumentos_de_ferramenta_test.exs (describe "RN-725": escape decoded, text without \u and list intact; refusal with the keys and without the values; complete_story names the field); apps/api/test/domain/backlog/story-overlap.spec.ts (escaped title matches the normal one; a different title stays different)
    • Origin: AT-405, AT-408

    RN-726 — The provider's exhausted credit pauses the dev agent, with origin infra, without burning the following tasks

    • Rule: when the model's provider refuses for lack of CREDIT (HTTP 402, or OpenRouter's text "available credits"/"add credits"), the dev agent's turn is not "stopped without concluding": the task is blocked with the reason "crédito do provider esgotado" and origin infra (never codigo), the automatic stop counter (RN-047) does NOT advance, and the agent does NOT claim the next task — it stays in idle_tripped and emits dev.credit_exhausted (motivo: credito_esgotado), never dev.idle_tripped. Resumption is the Rearmar that already existed in the team panel, after topping up the credit; the blocked task comes back through the usual unblock. The Executores screen shows the agent travado with "crédito do provedor do modelo esgotado — recarregue e rearme para retomar", and the bell says the same. The same recognition makes FalhaDeTurno.origem/1 return infra for the credit final frame (it applies to the gate agents, like QA). The session counts dev.credit_exhausted as pending work, like dev.idle_tripped. Since RN-730 the 402 arrives with its own code (insufficient_credit) and it is that which decides; recognition by text remained only as a safety net.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/agents/falha_de_turno.ex:142 (credito_esgotado?), apps/engine/lib/engine/dev/dev_agent_server.ex:902 (handle_outcome), apps/engine/lib/engine/dev/agent_io.ex:271 (pausar_por_credito), apps/web/src/lib/agent-status.ts:198 (breakerReasonFor), apps/api/src/application/use-cases/sessions/get-session-pending-work.use-case.ts
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/dev/dev_agent_server_test.exs ("402 do provider: origem infra, pausa sem idle_tripped e sem queimar a próxima task"; failure: "erro que não é de crédito segue como antes: parou sem concluir, conta no disjuntor"), apps/engine/test/engine/agents/falha_de_turno_test.exs ("402 do provider é infra e é reconhecido"), apps/web/src/lib/agent-status.test.ts ("RN-726: dev.credit_exhausted é travado com motivo de crédito…")
    • Origin: AT-407 (TP-01 of 10/03: OpenRouter's 402 became three tasks blocked with origin codigo and the automatic stop)

    RN-731 — The turn's closing states what was recorded with the server's number

    • Rule: the number of what a conversational agent recorded in a turn is a SERVER FACT, never model text (RN-163). The Creative, the PO and the Architect count, in the turn's process, each write tool that came back ok (emit_artifact by type — business rule, decision, note, other —, create_epic, create_story, update_story, archive_story, create_task), and the closing of agent.response gains, AFTER the model's text and without rewriting it, the line "Gravado neste turno: N …". A refused tool and a read do not count; a turn without writes gets no line; the count dies with the turn. The line comes out in the turn's language (RN-622): the author's, otherwise the project's; pt* in Portuguese ("Gravado neste turno: 1 regra de negócio"), any other language — or none resolved — in English ("Recorded this turn: 2 business rules"), with singular and plural in both.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/agents/gravado_no_turno.ex:62 (anotar), :95 (descarregar), apps/engine/lib/engine/harness/idioma_da_resposta.ex:272 (idioma_do_turno), apps/engine/lib/engine/agents/texto_do_turno.ex:65 (payload_do_turno)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/agents/gravado_no_turno_test.exs (happy: six rules and one decision recorded, one refused — the model's "8 regras" closing stays and gains "6 regras de negócio, 1 decisão" next to it; no writes: nothing added; pt-BR and en, singular and plural; a language outside the two falls back to en), apps/engine/test/engine/agents/criativo_server_test.exs
    • Origin: AT-414 (TP-01 of 10/03: the Creative closed with "Registrei as 8 regras de negócio" and the panel had 6)

    RN-732 — The turn text goes through the same escape ruler as the arguments

    • Rule: the text the model writes in the turn of a conversational agent — the content (closing) and each passos of the agent.response — goes through the SAME decodificar_escapes/1 of RN-725 before being written: a literal ã becomes ã. One ruler, no copy, and with the same limit: it only acts when there is a literal \uXXXX, so text without an escape — including code with a literal "\n" in a fenced block — comes back byte for byte. The streaming agent.delta is NOT decoded: an escape can arrive split across two deltas, and the written bubble replaces the running text at the end of the turn. A rule of its own, and not a clause of RN-725, because the surface is another (a model answer, not a tool argument) and the decision about the delta is this one's.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/agents/texto_do_turno.ex:65 (payload_do_turno), apps/engine/lib/engine/harness/argumentos_de_ferramenta.ex:102 (decodificar_escapes)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/agents/texto_do_turno_test.exs (happy: the five measured escapes, in the closing and in a step; intact: text without an escape and a code block with a literal \n)
    • Origin: AT-417 (TP-01 of 03/10, 2nd round: the Creative's final answer written with "colisão", "usuário" and three more escapes in the bubble)

    RN-733 — The credit failure says what to do, and the reason is never the tuple

    • Rule: the reason recorded in the agent.error of the conversational agents, the Infra Lead and the ToolLoop (dev agent) is the TEXT of the final frame when there is one — never the inspected {:final, …} tuple; other shapes keep inspect/1. With the provider's credit exhausted (errorCode: insufficient_credit, RN-730, or the text recognition of RN-726), the bubble's mensagem is short and actionable in the turn's language (RN-622): pt "Crédito do provedor do modelo esgotado — recarregue a chave e tente de novo.", any other language "The model provider's credit is exhausted — top up the key and try again."; with no resolved language, pt-BR. The provider's JSON stays only in the reason (diagnosis). The other errors keep the usual sentence.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/agents/falha_de_turno.ex:166 (mensagem), :189 (diagnostico)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/agents/dev_lead_server_test.exs (402 in pt-BR and en: short sentence, reason without {:final; upstream error: usual sentence), apps/engine/test/engine/agents/falha_de_turno_test.exs
    • Origin: AT-421 (TP-01 of 10/03, 2nd round: the Dev Lead recorded reason: "{:final, …" and the bubble dumped the 402's JSON)

    RN-735 — The none network of the Architect's image says it does not install dependencies

    • Rule: the description of choose_project_image and its RESULT say that, with network none, no package manager (npm, pnpm, pip, mix, cargo, go mod...) installs dependencies in the container, and that Infra inherits that network (RN-723). With none, the result asks the model to call the tool again with egress if the stack installs dependencies. It is a WARNING: the api does not guess the stack, and the broker's containment (ADR 0130) does not change. (Since RN-906 the api REFUSES none when a module's declared stack downloads dependencies; outside that case, only the warning stands.)
    • Code: apps/engine/lib/engine/harness/tools/choose_project_image.ex:96 (mensagem_de_fixada), apps/engine/lib/engine/harness/tools/choose_project_image.ex:23 (spec)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/harness/tools/choose_project_image_test.exs
    • Origin: AT-420 (TP-01 of 03/10: node:22-bookworm-slim with network none, and the dev agent could not npm install)

    RN-742 — The dev agent does not die silently, and the late HTTP response does not stay in its mailbox

    • Rule: every non-streamed HTTP call from the engine to the api (post_returning/3 and the GETs of EngineApiClient.Live) runs in a disposable Task; a response that arrives after the receive_timeout lands in a process that has already ended, never in the caller's mailbox, and an exception inside the request comes back as {:error, _}. And the dev agent that CRASHES (an exit that is neither :normal nor :shutdown) is not treated as finished: the Engine.Dev.Monitor writes a DURABLE agent.error with origin infra (reason: processo_do_dev_caiu, religado, taskId) and RESTARTS it from its own dev_agent_states row, up to 3 times per agent; the resume goes through the handle_continue({:restart_recovery, _}) that already existed, which blocks the interrupted task with a diagnosis (origin infra) and tries the next claim. With no row, or at the ceiling, the row goes away and the error says there was no restart. llm_turn_stream/6 stays out of the isolation (the into: writes to the caller's dictionary); the conversational agents already call it from inside the turn's Task (TurnoAssincrono), so the "HTTP inside the GenServer" pattern does not reach them — declared. The web did not change: the Executors tab now sees the recovery's agent.error and dev.blocked.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/sessions/engine_api_client.ex:2185 (isolado), apps/engine/lib/engine/dev/monitor.ex:69 (ao_terminar), :107 (registrar_e_religar)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/sessions/engine_api_client_isolado_test.exs (a late message does not reach the caller; an exception becomes {:error, _}), apps/engine/test/engine/dev/monitor_test.exs (a crash writes agent.error infra and restarts; :normal deletes the row; with no row it does not restart)
    • Origin: AT-428 (08/10: CaseClauseError in Finch.HTTP1.Conn.receive_response/8 after the npm install timeout)

    Revised by RN-922 (ADR 0226). A dev that crashes with a recent working and no earlier resumption on the task is no longer blocked: it preserves the worktree as a commit and resumes the same task once. Outside that, the block described above stands.

    RN-743 — Blocking the task preserves the worktree's work, and the next one starts from it

    • Rule: before blocking a task (any origin, including the taskBudgetMicros budget ceiling, whose VALUE does not change here), what the dev agent left in the worktree becomes a commit on the task's own branch, with the identity <agente>[bot] (git add -A + commit --no-verify). The dev.blocked carries trabalhoPreservado ({branch, commit} or null) and the diagnosis STATES where the work was left — or that it could not be preserved, with the reason; a failure to preserve never prevents the block. The SAME agent's next task (therefore, of the same module) is born from that branch (create_from/4) instead of dev, once only. A clean worktree produces no commit. Declared: the pointer lives only in memory (an engine restart loses the pointer, not the commit), and in runner mode the worktree is on the user's machine and the engine preserves nothing.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/dev/worktree_manager.ex:72 (preservar_em), :55 (create_from), apps/engine/lib/engine/dev/agent_io.ex:491 (preservar_trabalho), apps/engine/lib/engine/dev/agent_io.ex:321 (base_preservada)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/dev/worktree_manager_test.exs (preservar_em/3 e create_from/4: commit with dev-api[bot] and the next task sees the file; clean is :nada; a folder without a repository is an error), apps/engine/test/engine/dev/bloqueio_preserva_trabalho_test.exs (the dev.blocked states the branch; a failure to preserve does not prevent the block)
    • Origin: AT-429 (10/08: feature/task-86dade03 without a commit, package.json and src lost after the budget block)

    RN-744 — The dev knows the shell is sh and starts by seeing its own worktree

    • Rule: the terminal tool's description says the container's shell is sh (dash), not bash — no brace expansion, no [[ ]], no arrays, no source — and gives the form that works. Each dev agent task's kickoff gets a second pinned message with the worktree's SNAPSHOT: the branch and the files that already exist (tracked + untracked not ignored), with a ceiling of 80 lines and the real total when it cuts (ADR 0060); it is the server that assembles it, with no parameter from the model. A folder the engine cannot reach (runner mode) says that the snapshot is unavailable and says to list through terminal. Declared and NOT fixed: the dev's worktree may be born before the infra PR's merge, so what the Infra delivered through a PR may not be on the dev it starts from.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/harness/tools/terminal.ex:12 (spec), apps/engine/lib/engine/dev/worktree_manager.ex:108 (retrato), apps/engine/lib/engine/dev/dev_agent_server.ex:580 (retrato_do_worktree)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/dev/worktree_manager_test.exs (retrato/2: branch and files; an unreachable folder says unavailable; the terminal description says sh)
    • Origin: AT-444 (TP-01 of 10/08: mkdir -p src/{a,b} in dash and the dev recreating what already existed)

    RN-745 — In a cut answer, only the incomplete call is refused

    • Rule: when the model's answer comes cut by the output ceiling (truncated: true, RN-737), only the LAST tool call of the answer is treated as incomplete and refused with the named error (which now says "the last one of the answer", that the earlier ones were executed and asks for "one per call"). The earlier calls of the same answer execute normally — measured in the code: the OpenAI-compatible stream assembles the calls in order, the cut falls on the last one, and the api's parseArgumentos returns {} for the broken JSON. The call is identified by name and arguments. A cut answer without a known call still refuses every tool (RN-737's yardstick). The description of propose_adr steers one ADR per answer.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/harness/resposta_cortada.ex:44 (registrar), :64 (incompleta?), apps/engine/lib/engine/harness/argumentos_de_ferramenta.ex:26 (executar), apps/engine/lib/engine/harness/tools/propose_adr.ex:27 (spec)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/harness/resposta_cortada_test.exs ("só a última chamada da resposta cortada é recusada; as completas executam"; "ferramenta de resposta cortada NÃO executa", without a known call)
    • Origin: AT-430 (10/08: the Architect sent 5 propose_adr in a cut answer and all five were refused; 0 ADRs)

    RN-746 — The Architect's closing states what it recorded, and refused writes are counted

    • Rule: the factual line "Gravado neste turno: …" of RN-731 now counts the Architect's writes (proposed ADR, module map, module assignment, decided image, C4 diagram, routing to infra, module contract, insight), the Staff's RFC and the UX Designer's prototype, and counts separately the refused WRITE calls ({:error, _}) of the turn: "…; N chamadas de escrita recusadas". A turn that tried to write and recorded nothing closes with "Gravado neste turno: nada; N chamadas de escrita recusadas." (en: "Recorded this turn: nothing; N write calls refused."). The Creative now notes refusals too; Staff and UX Designer now note them. A read never counts. The model's text is not rewritten (RN-163: the agent does not announce as done what the server refused — the reader sees the fact next to it). Out, declared: Dev Lead (the plan suspends the turn, RN-284) and Infra Lead.

    RN-756 — The Anamnese only runs with the subject's technical-content interaction; approving does not count

    • Rule: revises the "own interaction" count of RN-680/RN-722. Only these count toward making someone a subject of the round: (a) an actor_kind: "user" event of the member themselves whose type is in tipos_de_evento_com_conteudo/0 — chat.message (a message written by them) and chat.structured_question_answered — and (b) an action decision with a written refusal reason (non-empty rejectionReason, a correction). Approval, handoff acceptance, a click and any other user event do NOT count: they are not evidence of competence. The floor of 5 (RN-722), the daily ceiling and the no-subject outcome (reason in the log; anamnese.run_skipped only in a manually requested round) do not change, and triage stays LOCAL, before the LLM and the RAG.

    • Origin: AT-439 (TP-01, 10/08, project c1dc053d: two rounds during execution, US$ 0.20, both skip_proficiency with "o usuário apenas aprovou propostas")

    RN-757 — The auto-approved terminal waits for the command, and the command ceiling is 2 min

    • Rule: the terminal action the api auto-approves is EXECUTED inside the same propose_action request (api → engine /internal/actions/execute → api container-exec → broker), and the engine waits for that request for TERMINAL_ACTION_TIMEOUT_MS + 120 s — not for the Req default of 15 s. The command ceiling (TERMINAL_ACTION_TIMEOUT_MS) goes from 15 s to 120 s in the three composes and in runtime.exs. The chain becomes broker (T) < api → broker (T + up to 45 s, RN-604) < engine → container-exec (T + 90 s) < engine → propose_action (T + 120 s), and with T = 120 s the outer hop (240 s) stays below the 300 s of headers of Node's fetch. If the wait still overflows, the model receives NAMED text — the ceiling in seconds, that the process may keep running and the suggestion of nohup … & with a log —, never a raw %Req.TransportError{reason: :timeout}. Asynchronous/streaming exec was considered and not done: the smallest change that makes a common npm install fit is the ceiling.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/sessions/engine_api_client.ex:1959 (opcoes_do_propose_action) and apps/engine/lib/engine/harness/hooks/action_pipeline.ex:85 (resultado_de_teto_do_terminal)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/sessions/engine_api_client_propose_terminal_test.exs (a fake api that takes 16 s is still waited for; order of the ceilings below 300 s; the overflow text names the ceiling)
    • Origin: AT-440 (TP-01 of 10/08: npm install 2>&1 | tail -50 in a node:22-bookworm-slim container came back "falha no pipeline: %Req.TransportError{reason: :timeout}"; measured on this machine, the same npm install of bcrypt+sqlite3+express took 5.3 s with the image in cache and prebuilds available — the chain, not the command, cut at 15 s)

    RN-760 — The dev agent's next task starts from its last not-yet-merged branch

    • Rule: generalizes RN-743. On finishing a task — approved in the gates (nextAction: "done") or blocked for ANY reason, including the "ciclo de correção esgotado (gate)" that the api decides and reaches the engine only as gate_resolved —, the dev agent keeps its branch and the SAME agent's next task (therefore, of the same module) is born from that branch, and not from dev. What was left uncommitted in the worktree is preserved first, as in RN-743; the pointer recorded by block_task/4 is not overwritten. When the previous branch is already an ancestor of the local dev (merged), the next one starts from dev, which already contains it. WHEN the queue moves does not change: the next task is still claimed at the end of the gates, before the user's merge (owner decision). Declared: a squash merge on a remote does not leave the branch an ancestor of dev, so the next one keeps starting from it; the pointer still lives only in memory, and in runner mode the merge check does not run.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/dev/agent_io.ex:296 (levar_branch_para_a_proxima), :324 (criar_worktree), apps/engine/lib/engine/dev/worktree_manager.ex:203 (ja_mergeada_volta_ao_trabalho)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/dev/bloqueio_preserva_trabalho_test.exs (levar_branch_para_a_proxima/1 e criar_worktree/2: approved carries the branch to the next one; the block's pointer is not overwritten), apps/engine/test/engine/dev/worktree_manager_test.exs (add_worktree/4 com a branch da task anterior: not merged starts from it, merged starts from dev)
    • Origin: AT-447 (TP-01 of 10/09: tasks 762833bd and 5ebad64b — the second was born from dev without the first's login route, and QA failed it)

    RN-763 — Closing the execution session stops the dev agents and the gates, and "Parar execução" in the Executores tab

    • Rule: closing the session (session.closed/closed_abnormally, through SessionLifecycleWorker, the point every closing goes through) STOPS its dev agents on all nodes and the project's gates (QA lead and SecOps) — the yardstick that already applied to the conversational agents (RN-581). The process is brought down immediately, without recording the turn in progress, and Engine.Dev.Monitor is warned first (esquecer/1): the durable row is removed and the agent is NOT restarted (RN-742). For each dev with a task in progress (working, awaiting_approval or awaiting_gate), the worktree's work becomes a commit on the task's branch (RN-743) and the task is BLOCKED with reason "sessão de execução encerrada", origin politica, and the diagnosis saying where the work was left — it goes back to the queue when a human releases it, never lost. The session's gate_states rows are removed, so the GateRescuer does not reopen a cycle of the closed session. The Executores tab gets "Parar execução" (role developer, that of closing a session), whose mechanism is the SAME closing (closing → closed), without a new state; the confirmation says before the click what remains: the tasks in progress (with the count), the open PRs, the pending approvals and that the session does not reopen. Declared: a dev agent's pending action stays pending; an open PR stays open; a session with execution.activated does not reopen (RN-650), so resuming means activating execution in a new session; the confirmation's task count comes from the tab's event window. The confirmation on the session bar's "Encerrar" belongs to another activity (AT-452).
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/dev/encerramento_da_sessao.ex:40 (parar_da_sessao), :74 (parar_processos), :104 (devolver_task), apps/engine/lib/engine/dev/monitor.ex:34 (esquecer), apps/engine/lib/engine/workers/session_lifecycle_worker.ex:94 (parar_execucao), apps/web/src/components/PararExecucao.tsx
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/dev/encerramento_da_sessao_test.exs (the dev dies without being restarted, the row is removed, the task is blocked with origin politica; a session without a dev is a no-op and a dev of another session stays up), apps/web/src/components/PararExecucao.test.tsx (the confirmation says what remains and closes closing → closed; the api's refusal stays in the confirmation; without the role the button is inert with the reason)
    • Origin: AT-456 (TP-01 of 10/09: with the execution session closed, the dev-encurtador-api blocked task 3, took 4 and opened a PR; the only brake was stopping the container)

    RN-765 — The QA gate judges the TASK, and a sibling task's RF is an observation

    • Rule: QA-strategy (test plan, RN-674) and QA-automation receive, besides the story, the task's CUT: title and description, the story's business rules and the OTHER tasks of the same story with their status (siblingTasks in GET .../dev-context). The verdict is about what belongs to THIS task: a requirement that belongs to a sibling task (done or pending) does not fail it — it goes to resumo as an observation, outside itens, and does not prevent approved. The yardstick is PROMPT, not code: emit_qa_verdict still requires only a green suite to approve. Since AT-479, when the task description or a rule quantifies PER item ("por X", "cada", "de todos os" — "por exemplo"/"por padrão" do not count), the cut gains the section "Forma da resposta": check that the answer carries the value of EACH item, not only the total (the dashboard approved with only {totalCliques} over "a contagem… de todos os links"), and that the input refusal names the field — the latter is an OBSERVATION in resumo, outside itens, unless a rule requires it. It remains a prompt yardstick: no new failure in code.

    RN-772 — The Architect reads the CURRENT status of the ADRs it proposed

    • Rule: the Architect has the read listar_adrs_propostas: the PROJECT's open_adr_pr actions, read now from proposed_actions (same Postgres, no HTTP in the loop), with each one's status — pending, approved, auto-approved, executed (with the PR URL), denied (with the reason) or failed. Contained (ADR 0060, RN-164): no parameter, scope closed to the context's project, one count and one query per call, the 20 most recent, and the real total stated when it cuts. A read failure is tool.result ok: false with the reason, never empty text. Since RN-784 it IS in the Jev's floor.

    RN-773 — The Architect reads the backlog, and checks before asserting state

    • Rule: the Architect gains the same listar_backlog as the PO (RN-164), with the task count per story.     RN-774 — The first task of each module gets double the per-task ceiling

    The first task of a module builds the whole skeleton (manifest, app, database, tests) and costs more than the following ones: in the TP-01 of 08/10 it spent US$ 0.53 with Haiku 4.5 and was blocked by the fixed ceiling of US$ 0.50 (taskBudgetMicros: 500000). By the owner's decision (09/10), it gets 2× the per-task ceiling (US$ 1.00 with the default); the others follow the configured ceiling. The multiplier is a named constant in the engine, not configuration.

    "First" is measured by the log, never by the task's state: it is the task whose backlog.task_claimed is the OLDEST in the project for that module. Blocking zeroes assigned_to and restarting the engine loses the process's memory; the log does not change, so the same task claimed again after being released is still the first, and a correction requested by a gate also gets the 2×. The api answers task.primeiraDoModulo in the dev's context (false without module, and false with no claim at all for the module — the normal ceiling is the conservative one), and the EFFECTIVE ceiling is resolved at a single point in the engine. The budget block states the ceiling that applied to that task ("teto da primeira tarefa do módulo: 1000000 micro-USD, US$ 1.00"). The QA gates keep the base task_budget_micros — outside the card's cut.

    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/dev/teto_da_tarefa.ex:32 (efetivo); apps/engine/lib/engine/dev/dev_agent_server.ex:718 (diagnostico_de_orcamento); apps/api/src/application/use-cases/execution/get-dev-task-context.use-case.ts:182 (ehPrimeiraTarefaDoModulo)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/dev/dev_agent_server_test.exs (describe "RN-774": the first with US$ 0.53 does not block, the second of the same module blocks — failure case —, the first of another module also gets 2×); apps/engine/test/engine/dev/teto_da_tarefa_test.exs; apps/api/test/application/use-cases/execution/get-dev-task-context.use-case.spec.ts (describe "RN-774")
    • Origin: AT-433

    RN-778 — The orphan dev agent closes by a new event at boot

    • Rule: at boot, for each non-terminal session, the dev agent whose last dev.* in the session is a waiting one (dev.working, dev.blocked, dev.idle_tripped and the others that GetSessionPendingWorkUseCase counts) and that has NO row in dev_agent_states in THIS session gets a dev.error with origin infra (reason: dev_agent_sem_processo) followed by dev.idle, which takes it out of the pending-work ruler (RN-064). It is the pattern of RN-586 for the dev.* vocabulary: it never re-executes, never rewrites an event, and the durable row is the proof of an owner — with it the DevRehydrator restarts the agent, and closing it would kill a healthy agent. It does not touch the task: a blocked task or one with a PR waiting for the merge keeps holding the current execution session through RN-776 (AT-457), which has a human owner.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/dev/dev_orfao.ex:49 (varrer), apps/engine/lib/engine/sessions/rehydrator.ex:81 (varrer)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/dev/dev_orfao_test.exs (the orphan closes with dev.error infra + dev.idle; the one with a durable row and the one that is already idle do not; a read that fails closes nothing)
    • Origin: AT-465 (TP-01 of 09/10: session 63a4e1aa rescheduled by the heartbeat every 30 s by a dev.working with no process)

    Cited by RN-922 (ADR 0226), unchanged: the dev with no durable row in dev_agent_states is still only closed, because there is no task to resume.

    RN-779 — The worktree of the (re)claimed task starts from the CURRENT dev and integrates it

    • Rule: before creating a task's worktree (first time or resumed), the engine runs git fetch origin in the project's working tree (serialized per project) — it is on the remote that PRs are merged, and the local dev only moved at the checkout of initialization. The tip of dev is origin/dev when the local one fell behind. The task is born: (1) from the previous task's unmerged branch (RN-760), (2) from its OWN branch when it has work that dev does not contain — the task resumed in a new process, which lost the in-memory pointer (RN-743) —, or (3) from the current tip of dev. In cases (1) and (2) the worktree INTEGRATES the tip of dev (merge --no-ff, identity <agente>[bot]) before the first step. A conflict in that integration goes to the agent (RN-1010); only what the agent does not resolve is a NAMED refusal ("conflito ao integrar a dev atual", with the files): the merge is aborted, the source branch stays intact and the task blocks with its own reason and origin codigo. A failing fetch blocks the task with git's reason, never falls back to the old dev. Readoption after a merge conflict (RN-715) does not integrate on its own. Measured in TP-01: AT-447 FIRED on the 1st claim (reflog: Created from feature/task-b3cc92b8); what lost the base was the reclaim, which reset the branch to the LOCAL dev stuck at the infra merge (Reset to dev). Declared: in runner mode none of this runs (the git is the user's machine's).
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/dev/worktree_manager.ex:155 (atualizar_remoto), :199 (add_worktree), :259 (ponta_do_trabalho), :203 (retomada), :332 (integrar_trabalho)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/dev/worktree_manager_test.exs (integração da dev atual (RN-779): resumed with an old branch starts from the remote's new dev; preserved work integrates the new dev; a previous one in awaiting_user is the base with dev integrated; a conflict is a named refusal)
    • Origin: AT-458 (TP-01 of 10/09: feature/task-c80daf6c was born from 1fa2241 without the 12 tasks merged afterwards, and the merge conflicted)

    RN-780 — A reasoning-only answer cut by the ceiling is a cut, not giving up

    • Rule: in the ToolLoop (the path of every execution and gate agent), an answer without text and without a tool call that the api marks truncated: true (RN-737's finish_reason: length) is reasoning that ate the output ceiling. The round is retried ONCE, without the empty answer in the history and counting an iteration — the iteration ceiling and the task budget still apply. If it repeats, the loop stops marking corte_pelo_teto, and the dev agent blocks the task with the reason "resposta só de raciocínio, cortada pelo teto" and origin modelo, instead of "parou sem concluir nem reportar bloqueio". A tool call in between resets the retry. Declared: the engine receives from the api truncated and usage.outputTokens, but NOT the reasoningTokens (they stay only in token_usage), so an empty answer that hits the ceiling without finish_reason: length is not recognized.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/harness/tool_loop.ex:256 (parada_sem_chamada), apps/engine/lib/engine/dev/dev_agent_server.ex:1089 (motivo_da_parada), :1209 (stop_diagnosis)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/harness/tool_loop_test.exs (corte só de raciocínio: retried once and goes on; repeated stops with corte_pelo_teto), apps/engine/test/engine/dev/dev_agent_server_test.exs (resposta só de raciocínio cortada pelo teto bloqueia com motivo nomeado)
    • Origin: AT-459 (TP-01 of 10/09: task c80daf6c blocked in 40 s with 8,192 output tokens, all of them reasoning)

    RN-781 — The dev's commit message does not record a pending item that belongs to the system

    • Rule: the report_done summary is the dev agent's commit message, and commit, push and PR are done by the system after it. Before proposing the git_commit, the engine cuts from the pending marker ("Pendente", "Pendência", "Pending") to the end when the rest speaks of commit, push or PR, and removes the parenthesis "(ainda sem commit…)"; a pending item that does not speak of that stays. The description of the summary field says it becomes the commit message and that commit, push and PR are not narrated. A summary that ends up empty falls back to the default message <agente>: <task>.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/dev/agent_io.ex:374 (mensagem_de_commit), :373 (propose_commit), apps/engine/lib/engine/dev/tools/report_done.ex:27 (summary)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/dev/mensagem_de_commit_test.exs (cuts the narration; removes the parenthesis; a pending item unrelated to the system stays)
    • Origin: AT-464 item 2 (TP-01 of 10/09: git log feature/task-9d6dcd53 with "Pendente: commit… as ações git_commit, git_push e pr_open não estavam disponíveis neste passo")

    RN-787 — The gate counts the PR's diff against the origin's dev, and the QA verdict records a list

    • Rule: (1) the diff that QA and SecOps judge (dev...HEAD, RN-664) uses the NEWEST dev the working tree sees: with a local origin (a path on disk) the gate first runs git fetch origin dev, best effort, and between origin/dev and the local dev the one that contains the other wins. The clone's local dev does not move when the PR is merged at the origin, and the SecOps verdict counted the accumulation of the already merged tasks. With a remote origin there is no fetch in the gate (the credential belongs to Workspace) — declared. The semgrep and gitleaks keep scanning the whole worktree, not only the diff: narrowing what the scanner sees changes what the gate fails, and that is left to the owner. (2) itens and coverageMatrix of the QA verdict reach artifact.qa_verdict always as a LIST: a JSON string of a list is decoded (the mold of RN-719), and a string that is not a list becomes [].
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/diff.ex:44 (base_mais_nova), apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/hooks/termination.ex:54 (lista)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/gates/diff_test.exs (task B born from A, with A merged at the origin: the diff is only B) and apps/engine/test/engine/gates/hooks/termination_test.exs (a JSON string becomes a list; a list intact; an invalid string becomes [])
    • Origin: AT-464 items 1 and 3 (TP-01 of 10/09, session 80e3ce09: "17 arquivo(s) alterado(s) nesta PR" in a PR of 3 files against dev, and coverageMatrix/itens recorded as "[]" in the last verdicts)

    RN-790 — The README on how to start the deliverable is a DoD criterion the PO writes

    • Rule: the PO's kickoff instruction says to include, in the DoD of the story that creates the application's entry point (server, CLI, configuration or persistence) — or in a story of its own —, the criterion "README diz como instalar, configurar (variáveis de ambiente obrigatórias e opcionais, com o padrão) e subir a aplicação, e como criar o primeiro acesso, quando houver". The dev agent fulfills the story's DoD, and the gate judges by it. A PROMPT yardstick, like RN-765: no tool refuses a story without that criterion. The other variant — the dev agent updating the README on every task that touches configuration/entry point — was NOT done: it would require classifying the task in AmbienteDoAgente or in report_done, a new code rule — declared.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/agents/po_server.ex:593 (kickoff_instruction)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/agents/po_server_test.exs:438 (the kickoff instruction carries the README criterion)
    • Origin: AT-466 (TP-01 of 10/09: encurtador-api came out with a README only with npm test, without the mandatory JWT_SECRET, DB_PATH/PORT, npm start nor how to create the first member)

    RN-792 — On declaring the contract, the Architect receives the stories' routes that the contract does not declare

    • Rule: after declare_module_contracts records the version (RN-684), the tool reads the project's backlog and extracts, deterministically and without an LLM, every MÉTODO /caminho (GET, POST, PUT, PATCH, DELETE) from the title, description, RF and DoD of the non-archived stories, and the same pattern from the contract's assinaturas. A route cited by a story and absent from the UNION of the contract's routes (the front story cites the back route) comes back in the result as a DIVERGENCE, with what the contract declares for the same method — loop input (RN-163), for the Architect to fix the contract or record the decision. Parameter (:id, {id}), trailing slash and query string are normalized; a ceiling of 10 lines, with the total. A failing backlog read does NOT refuse the declaration (already recorded): the result says it did not check. Left out, declared: a route only the contract declares (not a divergence), a story edited AFTER the declaration (only checked on the next one), and the contract following a route the DEV renamed (QA names the divergence in the verdict, RN-786, but nothing takes it back to the Architect).
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/harness/rotas_do_contrato.ex:37 (divergencias), apps/engine/lib/engine/harness/tools/declare_module_contracts.ex:125 (conferencia_com_historias)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/harness/tools/declare_module_contracts_test.exs (GET /painel in the story × GET /panel in the contract flags; an equal route with normalized parameter and slash does not flag; an unreadable backlog says it did not check)
    • Origin: AT-468 (TP-01 of 10/09: the story asked for GET /painel, the contract declared GET /panel, and nobody flagged it in the architecture)

    RN-796 — The gitleaks item states the rule and the excerpt with the secret masked

    • Rule: the item gitleaks puts in the SecOps verdict carries, besides the path and the line, the rule that flagged it (RuleID) and the flagged excerpt (Match) with the secret (Secret) replaced by *** — never the secret in clear. Without the Secret to mask, the excerpt does not go in. And the execution agent's environment (RN-706) says in one line that a test password, token or key is generated at runtime, never as a literal. No new allowlist.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/actions/gitleaks_detector.ex:121 (mensagem), apps/engine/lib/engine/harness/ambiente_do_agente.ex:102 (montar)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/actions/gitleaks_relatorio_test.exs, apps/engine/test/engine/harness/ambiente_do_agente_test.exs
    • Origin: AT-470

    RN-797 — A task whose branch has no diff against dev closes as "already in dev"

    • Rule: on report_done, before proposing commit, push and PR, the dev agent asks whether the worktree's branch carries something dev does not have: a clean tree and git diff --quiet <ponta da dev>...HEAD with no difference. It is the case of the reintegrated task (RN-779) whose code already came in through another PR. Without a diff, the task closes as done with the dev.task_already_in_dev event (agent, task, branch, reason) — no commit, no PR, no gates and no click. Any doubt (a folder the engine cannot reach, as in runner mode; git that fails; missing dev ref) is "has a diff": the task goes on to the gates, it never closes on its own. It measures DIFF and not ancestry, so it also covers the squash merge that RN-760 declares it does not see. Declared: the branch's old PR, if it exists, is not closed on the provider.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/dev/worktree_manager.ex:278 (sem_diff_contra_a_dev?), apps/engine/lib/engine/dev/dev_agent_server.ex:1122 (ja_na_dev)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/dev/tarefa_ja_na_dev_test.exs
    • Origin: AT-474

    RN-798 — QA checks the commands of the README touched by the delivery against the scripts

    • Rule: when the delivery's diff against dev touches a README, QA checks, without an LLM, that every npm run <s>, npm test, npm start, pnpm [run] <s> and yarn [run] <s> cited in it exists in scripts of the package.json of the same folder (otherwise the one at the worktree root). The check enters consolidation as one more verdict ("Conferência do README"): a divergence is changes_requested with one item per command, naming the missing script; with no divergence, it approves. Without a README in the diff, a diff that failed or an unreachable package.json, nothing is asserted. OUT, declared: the manager's built-in subcommand (pnpm install, pnpm dlx…), a command with a flag before the script (pnpm --filter x test), the project's own CLI flags and a command that is not Node's.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/conferencia_do_readme.ex:28 (conferir), apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/qa_lead_server.ex:818 (conferencia_do_readme)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/gates/conferencia_do_readme_test.exs
    • Origin: AT-475

    RN-805 — The recorded test plan is the one the tool accepted

    • Rule: emit_plano_de_teste validates the ALREADY normalized arguments (RN-719: criteriosExecutaveis in a JSON string becomes a list), and the hook that ends the QA-strategy's loop reads criteriosExecutaveis through the SAME normalization (Termination.lista/1, that of RN-787). Before, the tool said "plano registrado: 9 critérios", the hook carried the raw string, the artifact was refused (:criterios_vazios), the agent returned {:ok, plano} anyway and the Automation fell into the string's Enum — the QA Lead died and the gate stayed stopped until the rescue (18 min in real use). A refused artifact is now a NAMED failure of the QA-strategy (agent.error, origin codigo) and the review goes on without a plan, as it already did when the plan fails; the Automation also normalizes the criteria it receives.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/hooks/termination_plano_de_teste.ex:23 (criterios_executaveis), apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/qa_estrategia_agent.ex:231 (emit_returning)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/gates/qa_estrategia_agent_test.exs ("critérios em string JSON viram lista: o plano é gravado e devolvido como lista")
    • Origin: AT-478 (TP-01 of 10/10)

    RN-806 — One QA cycle per task: a request prior to the verdict is discarded

    • Rule: the QA Lead runs ONE cycle per task. A {:run, task} request for the task whose cycle is suspended waiting for a decision is ignored, and at the end of a cycle (verdict or block) every {:run, task} that was waiting in the process's mailbox is discarded — it was requested before the verdict existed, and the dev's legitimate correction is only born after it. In real use the rescue (RN-722) fired the cycle twice at the same instant, and the second, running after the approved one, recorded a contrary verdict about the already merged task. OUT, declared: two engine replicas (the mailbox is per process, the same caveat as the GateRescuer) and the cause of the double sweep of the rescue, which was not fixed here.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/qa_lead_server.ex:106 (handle_cast), apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/qa_lead_server.ex:157 (descartar_pedidos_obsoletos)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/gates/qa_lead_server_test.exs ("pedido repetido que esperava na caixa é descartado ao fim do ciclo"; "pedido para a task com ciclo suspenso não abre um segundo")
    • Origin: AT-478 (TP-01 of 10/10)

    RN-810 — A transient provider network failure is retried, and its block is infra without counting on the breaker

    • Rule: when the model call comes back with a provider NETWORK error — the code connection/timeout that the api normalizes (ADR 0041) or, with no code, the transport text (EAI_AGAIN, ECONNRESET, ETIMEDOUT, ECONNREFUSED, ENOTFOUND) —, the ToolLoop retries it up to twice, waiting 1 s and 3 s, writing toolloop.network_retry for each. It holds for every consumer of the ToolLoop (dev agents and gate subagents). Once the ceiling is exhausted, the origin of the outcome is infra, never codigo, and the dev agent's task block for this cause does NOT increment the counter of consecutive blocks (RN-047): the agent keeps claiming. In real use a 17 s getaddrinfo EAI_AGAIN openrouter.ai blocked two tasks as codigo and disarmed the dev. The post-gate correction follows the same ruler (AT-621, RN-864).
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/harness/tool_loop.ex:285 (llm_turn_com_retentativa), apps/engine/lib/engine/agents/falha_de_turno.ex:119 (falha_de_rede?), apps/engine/lib/engine/dev/dev_agent_server.ex:1043 (bloquear_parada), apps/engine/lib/engine/dev/agent_io.ex:228 (finish_task)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/harness/tool_loop_test.exs ("falha de rede do provider: retenta e segue quando a rede volta"; "falha de rede que persiste esgota o teto"), apps/engine/test/engine/dev/dev_agent_server_test.exs ("erro de rede do provider: origem infra e o disjuntor não conta"), apps/engine/test/engine/agents/falha_de_turno_test.exs
    • Origin: AT-484 (TP-01 of 10/10)

    RN-811 — "Already in dev" compares against the remote's updated dev

    • Rule: before the "already in dev" check (RN-797) on report_done, the dev agent updates the remote's dev through the SAME fetch that RN-779 does on claim, serialized per project. Without it the comparison used the dev of the day the worktree was born: the branch of a task whose commits had already entered the remote dev through another task's PR went through QA and SecOps with 0 files. A failing fetch decides nothing — it compares with what there is, and when in doubt the task goes on to the gates.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/dev/worktree_manager.ex:278 (sem_diff_contra_a_dev?), apps/engine/lib/engine/dev/worktree_manager.ex:301 (sem_diff_atualizado?), apps/engine/lib/engine/dev/dev_agent_server.ex:903 (handle_outcome)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/dev/tarefa_ja_na_dev_test.exs ("caminho feliz: com a dev atualizada, a branch não tem diff"; "falha: remoto inalcançável não fecha a task sozinha")
    • Origin: AT-486 (TP-01 of 10/10)

    RN-813 — A task that touches the entry point requires evidence that it starts

    • Rule: extension of the cut of RN-765. When the task's title or description touches the entry point, the wiring (routes, migration, registration) or the run README, the QA gate's cut gains the section "Ponto de entrada": require evidence that the REAL entrypoint starts (the project's start/main run, or a test that imports the real entrypoint, not an app assembled in the test itself) and responds on a route or command. Without that evidence, the gap goes to itens. It is a PROMPT yardstick: the gates' external contract (apps/api/config/gates.yml) and the emit_qa_verdict code do not change, and no new gate is born. A task that touches none of that does not get the section.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/recorte_da_tarefa.ex:121 (secao_do_ponto_de_entrada)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/gates/recorte_da_tarefa_test.exs ("tarefa que toca a fiação exige evidência do ponto de entrada real" and "tarefa que não toca entrypoint nem fiação não ganha a seção")
    • Origin: AT-483 (TP-01 of 10/10: server.ts never called the migration of the links table and /:code shadowed GET /links; the 53 tests passed because each one assembled its own database and app)

    RN-814 — The QA verdict lists all the gaps in one round

    • Rule: extension of the cut of RN-765: the QA gate lists in itens ALL the task's gaps it finds in the round, at once, never one per round — each round is a paid correction and a step of the cycle's ceiling. The previous prompt only said to list "which rules are missing", and a gap that was not an uncovered rule was left for the next round. Prompt yardstick.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/recorte_da_tarefa.ex:20 (texto)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/gates/recorte_da_tarefa_test.exs ("o recorte pede todas as lacunas numa volta só")
    • Origin: AT-487 (TP-01 of 10/10, task c7ced5a2: two failures for two gaps that already existed in the original delivery)

    RN-817 — QA runs the suite in a clean state, and a skipped test is not green

    • Rule: extension of the cut of RN-765, for every task. Before running the suite, the QA gate shuts down what the project ITSELF left up (the start's server listening on a project port), only processes from that folder, never outside ones. A test that starts a process must terminate the GROUP (or start node directly, not via npm); one that does not terminate hangs the suite in a clean clone and is a gap in itens. A test that SKIPPED itself due to environment (port in use, missing tool) does not count as green: it goes to resumo as an observation. It is an instruction of the cut, not a harness step: the one who runs the suite is QA itself through terminal, which already goes through the policy and the container routing of the three modes — a pkill in the harness would need a second execution path per mode. Prompt yardstick; the gates' contract does not change.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/recorte_da_tarefa.ex:178 (secao_do_ambiente_limpo)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/gates/recorte_da_tarefa_test.exs ("RN-817: pede ambiente limpo, encerrar o grupo e pulado como observação")
    • Origin: AT-496 (TP-01 of 10/10: npm test hung in a clean clone — the test killed npm and the grandchild node stayed on 3000 —, and in the container QA saw exit 0 because the test skipped itself with the port taken by an orphan server)

    RN-818 — The entry point evidence is the one the dev's environment produces

    • Rule: extension of RN-813. The "Ponto de entrada" section says what COUNTS as evidence in the dev's environment — starting the entrypoint's process in the project folder, running the script in the project container, hitting a route of the running process — and what does NOT fit there: building a Docker image, docker compose, external network. A requirement of that second kind never goes to itens; it goes to resumo as an observation. Prompt yardstick.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/recorte_da_tarefa.ex:121 (secao_do_ponto_de_entrada)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/gates/recorte_da_tarefa_test.exs ("RN-818: com ponto de entrada diz o que conta e o que não cabe" and "RN-818: sem ponto de entrada não traz a régua de evidência")
    • Origin: AT-492 (TP-01 of 10/10, task c708f8b2: the 4th round required building the image from the Dockerfile, the project container has no Docker, and the cycle ran out in a politica block)

    RN-819 — QA sweeps rule by rule before emitting

    • Rule: extension of RN-814. Besides listing all the gaps, the cut asks for a SWEEP BY RULE before emitting: go through each business rule and each criterion of the task, one by one, pointing out where the proof is or that it is missing, and only emit after going through all of them. "List all" alone did not prevent a pre-existing gap appearing in the next round. Prompt yardstick.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/recorte_da_tarefa.ex:20 (texto)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/gates/recorte_da_tarefa_test.exs ("RN-819: pede varredura por regra antes de emitir")
    • Origin: AT-490 (TP-01 of 10/10: tasks 2 and 11 brought in the 2nd round a gap that was already in the delivery)

    RN-823 — A gate cycle killed by an engine restart is resumed at boot, and the thread says so

    • Rule: at engine BOOT, the RECENT in_progress row of gate_states (below gate_rescue_stale_after_seconds, 15 min, and therefore invisible to the tick) with no live process on THIS node or on the OTHER nodes of the cluster is an orphan of the restart, not a slow cycle: the boot resumes it at once (Dispatcher.run_qa/run_secops), without waiting for the threshold. "Alive" is the ruler of RN-722 (the task in flight in the lead), also asked of the other nodes through :erpc.multicall — a node that does not answer counts as ALIVE, the ruler of TurnoOrfao (RN-586). The usual guards apply: a pending action from a gate actor does not restart, and an absent owner dev only deletes the row. Before re-dispatching, the boot writes gate.interrupted to the session log (taskId, gate, ultimaAtividadeEm, retomadoEm), and the thread shows it as a notice, in both languages. The periodic tick does NOT change: outside the boot, a recent row is a cycle in flight. Declared residue: a node that has not yet joined the cluster at the instant of the boot is not asked, and the worst outcome is the usual one (duplicated and cheap work; the second verdict is refused by the api).
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/gate_rescuer.ex:231 (run_boot), :240 (interrompido_no_boot), apps/web/src/routes/RetomarGateEstacionado.tsx:33 (cicloInterrompido)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/gates/gate_rescuer_test.exs ("boot com ciclo RECENTE órfão do reinício: grava gate.interrupted e retoma já (RN-823)"; failures: "boot com ciclo recente esperando ação PENDENTE do gate: não reinicia nem grava (RN-823)" and the live local process untouched), apps/web/src/routes/RetomarGateEstacionado.test.tsx ("cicloInterrompido (RN-823)")
    • Origin: AT-495 (TP-01 of 10/10: the 18:51 restart killed the QA cycle of task 10, and the dev stayed awaiting_gate for 15 min with no screen saying so)

    Revised by RN-923 (ADR 0226). The boot's resumption counts as the cycle's automatic one (auto_resumes): if the cycle has already used its own, the boot parks it instead of re-dispatching it, and the resumed subagent carries the plan from the last agent.turn_resumed.

    RN-832 — The dev agent knows report_done does commit, push and PR, and that a new test goes into the test script

    • Rule: the dev agent neither has nor needs git_commit, git_push or pr_open: commit, push and PR happen when it calls report_done. The description of report_done, that of report_blocked ("missing git tool" is not a reason to block), the dev agent's kickoff and the environment message (RN-706) say so — the environment one said, until now, to use the typed git actions, which the dev does not have. The kickoff and the environment also say that, if the test's package.json script lists files one by one, every new test file goes into it. Text only: no new code rule, and the Jev stays without a floor for dev agents (RN-758) — until RN-839, which put report_done/report_blocked in their floor.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/dev/tools/report_done.ex:16 (spec), apps/engine/lib/engine/dev/tools/report_blocked.ex:12 (spec), apps/engine/lib/engine/dev/dev_agent_server.ex:580 (initial_message), apps/engine/lib/engine/harness/ambiente_do_agente.ex:102 (montar)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/dev/report_done_instrucao_test.exs ("caminho feliz: report_done, kickoff e ambiente carregam as duas instruções"; failure: "o ambiente não manda mais usar as ações tipadas de git")
    • Origin: AT-499 (TP-01, 11th: a task blocked with the suite green for "git_commit não existe"; four QA rounds for a test outside the test script)

    RN-845 — The proposed ADRs read says whether the PR has already been merged

    • Rule: on the row of an executed ADR, listar_adrs_propostas (RN-772) no longer asserts "PR aberta" from the ACTION's status: it crosses the result's pullRequestId with the EXECUTED git_merge of the same project (the action every merge goes through, RN-418) and says "PR MERGEADA em <data da decisão>" or "PR aberta (nenhum merge dela registrado no Brabo)". A result without a PR id, or a merge query that fails, becomes "estado da PR: NÃO CONSEGUI LER" — never "aberta". Contained (ADR 0060): one extra query, only over the listed ids, in the same Postgres. Declared limit: a PR closed without a merge OUTSIDE the Brabo (on the remote provider) is not seen, and keeps being read as open — asking the provider would be HTTP in the agent's loop.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/harness/tools/listar_adrs_propostas.ex:88 (cruzar_com_merges), :102 (merges_por_pr), :155 (estado_da_pr).
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/harness/tools/listar_adrs_propostas_test.exs ("PR mergeada aparece como mergeada…", "estado da PR ilegível diz que não leu, nunca aberta").
    • Origin: AT-514 (TP-01, 10/11: the Architect said "aberta como PR" 30 s after the merge).

    RN-846 — The HTTP route goes into the README and the input error is the project's

    • Rule: extension of RN-819. The execution dev agent's kickoff asks that an HTTP route created or changed go into the README (method, path, body, responses and codes) and that an input error (malformed JSON, invalid field) respond with its own message in the project's format, never the library's raw message. The QA cut checks both things in the sweep by rule, and whatever is missing goes to itens. Prompt yardstick.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/dev/dev_agent_server.ex:580 (initial_message) and apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/recorte_da_tarefa.ex:20 (texto)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/dev/report_done_instrucao_test.exs ("RN-846: kickoff pede a rota no README e erro de entrada próprio") and apps/engine/test/engine/gates/recorte_da_tarefa_test.exs ("RN-846: confere rota no README e erro de entrada próprio")
    • Origin: AT-515 (TP-01, 12th: the README did not document the API, and malformed JSON responded 400 with the parser's "Expected property name…")

    RN-849 — The architecture readiness turn opens with the current state of the ADRs

    • Rule: extension of RN-845. When the user confirms that the architecture is ready, the Architect's server READS the ADRs' state through the same code as listar_adrs_propostas (local Postgres, no HTTP, same ceiling of 20 lines) and puts it in that turn's opening message, with the instruction not to assert a different ADR state. A failing read says "NÃO CONSEGUI LER" and says to state that it does not know — never "pendente".
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/harness/tools/listar_adrs_propostas.ex:50 (contexto_de_prontidao), apps/engine/lib/engine/agents/arquiteto_server.ex:223 (contexto_de_prontidao).
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/harness/tools/listar_adrs_propostas_test.exs ("contexto_de_prontidao/2 (RN-849)").
    • Origin: AT-519 (TP-01: in the readiness turn the Architect said "pendente de aprovação" about an already merged ADR, repeating the previous turn's read).

    RN-850 — The route goes into the "Rotas HTTP" section and the README carries no process note

    • Rule: extension of RN-846. The dev agent's kickoff asks that the route go INSIDE the README's "Rotas HTTP" section (created before "Testes" if missing), never at the end of the file, and that the README, which is the user's, not get a process note nor instructions for agents. The QA cut checks both things. Prompt yardstick.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/dev/dev_agent_server.ex:580 (initial_message) and apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/recorte_da_tarefa.ex:20 (texto)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/dev/report_done_instrucao_test.exs ("RN-850: a rota vai dentro de Rotas HTTP e o README não leva nota de processo") and apps/engine/test/engine/gates/recorte_da_tarefa_test.exs ("RN-850: confere a seção Rotas HTTP e a ausência de nota de processo")
    • Origin: AT-520 (TP-01: each task added routes at the end of the README, after "Testes", and a process paragraph for agents was left over)

    RN-851 — The Infra's compose starts the APP on the user's machine, and the PR declares how to run it

    • Rule: the Infra Lead's kickoff asks for the compose that STARTS THE APP (not only a dev environment): build of each module's Dockerfile with FROM = the decided image (projectImage of the Infra's context; without it, the routing's candidate, stated in the README), port ${APP_PORT:-8080}:<porta interna>, a volume for persistent data, a committed .env.example (never .env) and the section "Rodar na sua máquina" in the README. The agents' WORK container still starts through the broker, and there is no deploy. propose_infra_pr gets the MANDATORY field run (command up to 300 characters; urls from 1 to 5, label up to 60, kind in api_docs | web, url starting with http://localhost: or http://127.0.0.1:; boolean envExample), validated in the engine BEFORE the HALT with a named refusal that enters the loop (RN-163) and passed on in the open_infra_pr payload. Decision in ADR 0213.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/infra/instrucoes_de_execucao.ex:27 (validar), apps/engine/lib/engine/infra/infra_lead_server.ex:1414 (build_kickoff) and :1327 (abrir_pr), and apps/engine/lib/engine/infra/tools/propose_infra_pr.ex:24 (spec)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/infra/infra_lead_server_test.exs (describe "o compose sobe o app e a PR leva run (RN-851)")
    • Origin: AT-517 (the deliverable had no way to be run by the user)

    RN-853 — The dev exposes OpenAPI and Swagger when the task touches routes

    • Rule: part of ADR 0213. When the task touches an entry point or routes (the SAME ruler as the QA cut, toca_ponto_de_entrada?, never a copy), the dev's kickoff gains the clause: a module that exposes HTTP serves /openapi.json (OpenAPI 3) and /docs (Swagger UI); with no network or no package, /docs is an HTML page that loads swagger-ui from a CDN; a web module serves the home page; the URL goes into the "Rodar na sua máquina" section of the README. A task that does not touch routes does not pay for the clause (the message is pinned and returns on every iteration).
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/dev/dev_agent_server.ex:691 (clausula_de_openapi), apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/recorte_da_tarefa.ex:113 (toca_ponto_de_entrada?)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/dev/report_done_instrucao_test.exs ("RN-853: tarefa que toca rotas pede /openapi.json e /docs"; failure: "RN-853 falha: tarefa sem rota nem entrypoint não paga a cláusula")
    • Origin: AT-517

    RN-854 — QA proves /openapi.json and /docs inside the container

    • Rule: part of ADR 0213. In the entry point section of the cut (RN-813), QA starts the app with the README's command in the background INSIDE the container, hits localhost:<porta interna>/openapi.json and /docs (curl, wget or node -e fetch) and terminates the process group (RN-817). A failure goes to itens; the real docker compose up stays in resumo as "proven only on the user's machine".
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/recorte_da_tarefa.ex:121 (secao_do_ponto_de_entrada)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/gates/recorte_da_tarefa_test.exs ("tarefa que toca rotas pede /openapi.json e /docs, compose em resumo"; failure: "falha: tarefa sem rota não recebe a seção")
    • Origin: AT-517

    RN-862 — The open question on the engine side: tool, verdict and decision in the context

    • Rule: the Creative, the PO, the Architect and the Dev Lead declare every doubt left for the user with declare_open_question (pergunta up to 500, sugestao up to 300 optional, afeta: {regras, historias, tarefas}), which records artifact.open_question with questionId (uuid), autor and sessionId; an invalid payload is a named refusal and records nothing. The instruction is ONE central sentence, in the flow text of the four (RN-711), never per server. In the QA gate, a requirement "not confirmed by the user" (or contradicted by a current decision) is needs_user_decision, never changes_requested: emit_qa_verdict accepts it only with pergunta or openQuestionId, the QA Lead consolidates it above approved, the verdict goes to the api with the question and the cycle stays PARKED (ADR 0207, gate.rescue_parked, without automatic rescue) until the api asks for resumption through the RN-724 route. The dev-context decisoes enter the dev's kickoff and the QA cut as "Decisões do usuário — vencem RF/DoR quando conflitam" (pergunta → decisao); empty writes nothing.
