The recorded question counts in the turn's closing (RN-731), and the PO passes question_id in update_story/complete_story when it corrects the story from an answer (ADR 0214).
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/harness/tools/declare_open_question.ex:54 (run), apps/engine/lib/engine/harness/fluxo_do_time.ex:113 (frase_da_pergunta), apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/tools/emit_qa_verdict.ex:81 (run), apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/qa_lead.ex:89 (consolidar_decisao), apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/qa_lead_server.ex:595 (estacionar_por_decisao), apps/engine/lib/engine/dev/context_builder.ex:121 (secao_de_decisoes)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/harness/perguntas_em_aberto_test.exs ("válida grava artifact.open_question…"; "inválida é recusa nomeada…"; "consolidação: needs_user_decision vence approved"; "o recorte diz a régua…"; "ContextBuilder: a história leva as decisões…"), apps/engine/test/engine/gates/qa_lead_server_test.exs ("grava a pergunta na api, estaciona o ciclo e só o gesto humano o retoma")
    • Origin: AT-521, ADR 0214

    RN-863 — api_docs is always /docs, every route enters the OpenAPI, and QA fails the missing one

    • Rule: an HTTP module ALWAYS declares run.urls in /docs (the Swagger UI, kind: "api_docs") even before it exists, and a web module the home page — never a business route; the Infra's kickoff says so in the README step (extends RN-851). The run validation refuses, named and with the SAME yardstick on both sides, an api_docs whose URL does not end in /docs (extends RN-852). The dev's clause says that EVERY HTTP route created or changed in the task enters /openapi.json — path with method, body and responses (extends RN-853). In the QA cut, each route of the README's "Rotas HTTP" section must be in paths of the served /openapi.json; a missing route is an item that fails it, never an observation (extends RN-854).
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/infra/instrucoes_de_execucao.ex:60 (url), apps/api/src/domain/infra/run-instructions.ts:77 (recusaDoRun), apps/engine/lib/engine/infra/infra_lead_server.ex:1414 (build_kickoff), apps/engine/lib/engine/dev/dev_agent_server.ex:691 (clausula_de_openapi), apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/recorte_da_tarefa.ex:20 (texto)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/infra/infra_lead_server_test.exs ("run api_docs em rota de negócio é recusado com motivo, sem propor"), apps/api/test/application/use-cases/execution/get-run-instructions.use-case.spec.ts ("recusaDoRun (RN-852)"), apps/engine/test/engine/dev/report_done_instrucao_test.exs ("RN-853: …"), apps/engine/test/engine/gates/recorte_da_tarefa_test.exs ("RN-854: …")
    • Origin: AT-524, ADR 0213

    RN-864 — A transient network failure comes back on its own in up to 3 attempts, and the dev pauses

    • Rule: the task blocked by the dev agent with origin infra for a TRANSIENT network/provider failure — an explicit allowlist: DNS (EAI_AGAIN, ENOTFOUND, getaddrinfo), ECONNRESET, ETIMEDOUT, aborted, provider HTTP 5xx/429 (codes rate_limit/upstream, connection/timeout) — is RESUMED on its own up to 3 times, after 1, 3 and 10 min (the owner's decision), by an Oban job that calls the SAME unblock as the human (UnblockTaskUseCase, through the internal route .../tasks/:taskId/unblock, actor system, a no-op if the task is no longer blocked). The block reason says which resumption was scheduled; after the 3rd resumption the next failure stays blocked as before, and the reason says the 3 were already tried. And the agent PAUSES for 1 min instead of taking the next task (the wake is ignored during the pause). Exhausted credit (402, RN-726/730) and code errors do NOT apply: they block at once. The resumption counter is memory-only: restarting the engine zeroes it. It also holds for the post-gate correction (AT-621): the provider's 503 on a correction follows the same path as the main loop (credit RN-726 + resumption), with the reason marked "(correção)".
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/dev/dev_agent_server.ex:815 (handle_correction_outcome), apps/engine/lib/engine/agents/falha_de_turno.ex:140 (passageira?), apps/engine/lib/engine/dev/dev_agent_server.ex:1062 (bloquear_passageira), apps/engine/lib/engine/dev/agent_io.ex:293 (pausar_por_rede), apps/engine/lib/engine/workers/retomada_de_tarefa_worker.ex:33 (agendar), apps/api/src/application/use-cases/execution/unblock-task.use-case.ts:33 (ator)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/dev/dev_agent_server_test.exs ("falha passageira: agenda a retomada, pausa e não queima a fila", "fim da pausa volta a reivindicar"; failure: "falha passageira depois de 3 retomadas fica bloqueada pedindo o clique", "402 não agenda retomada"; correction: "correção com 503: infra, retomada agendada, pausa e disjuntor intacto", "correção sem crédito: pausa por crédito, sem retomada", "correção com erro não passageiro: bloqueia na hora, sem retomada"), apps/engine/test/engine/workers/retomada_de_tarefa_worker_test.exs
    • Origin: AT-525

    RN-865 — Endpoint and HTTP verb with a path also switch on the OpenAPI check, and it covers the code's routes

    • Rule: the single yardstick of "the task touches the entry point/routes" (toca_ponto_de_entrada?, used by the QA cut and by the dev's clause of RN-853) also matches "endpoint" and an HTTP verb followed by a path (POST /links). Without it, a task described as "Endpoint protegido…" received neither the OpenAPI clause (dev) nor the /openapi.json check (QA) — that is how POST /links and GET /panel stayed out of the OpenAPI with both tasks approved in TP-01. The QA check now requires in paths of the served /openapi.json, besides the routes of the README's "Rotas HTTP" section, every route the CODE registers; a missing one is an item that fails it (extends RN-863).
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/recorte_da_tarefa.ex:103 (@ponto_de_entrada), apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/recorte_da_tarefa.ex:114 (toca_ponto_de_entrada?)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/gates/recorte_da_tarefa_test.exs ("RN-865: tarefa que diz só endpoint ou verbo com caminho recebe a seção"; failure: "falha: tarefa sem rota não recebe a seção")
    • Origin: AT-527

    RN-868 — The gate rescue tick is a single chain

    • Rule: GateRescueSchedulerWorker reschedules its own successor with the SAME uniqueness as kickoff/0 (states available/scheduled/retryable, without executing, a window of two intervals). Before, only kickoff/0 was unique: the job in execution does not count, so the boot's kickoff with the previous tick in flight, or a retry that reruns perform/1, opened a SECOND chain, and they never merged. Measured in real use (10/14): 11 jobs scheduled at the same time and the RN-722 "espera decisão humana" line 11 times per tick for ONE gate_states row. It was not a data defect. With uniqueness on rescheduling the duplicate chains collapse into one on the next tick.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/workers/gate_rescue_scheduler_worker.ex:42 (unicidade)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/workers/gate_rescue_scheduler_worker_test.exs ("o tick se reagenda e mantém a corrente viva"; failure: "com um sucessor já agendado, o tick não abre uma segunda corrente")
    • Origin: AT-529

    RN-874 — The PO receives in the turn the user's decisions and the project's open questions

    • Rule: every PO turn carries, as an EPHEMERAL system message appended by the EngineApiClient facade right after the team flow (RN-711) — never in state.messages nor in the server's prompt —, the user's decisions (artifact.decision_record, choice and context) and the STILL open questions of the project (artifact.open_question without open_question.answered with the same questionId), from ALL sessions. The text says to write the decided ANSWER into the story, rule, DoR, DoD and task, never "pergunta em aberto N"; a still open question is cited by its questionId, without inventing an answer. The read is LOCAL (direct Postgres, without HTTP in the loop), of the 200 most recent events of those types, with a ceiling of 10 decisions, 10 questions, 240 characters per item and 3,000 in the text. Without a decision or an open question, or with the read failing, the turn goes on without the message, never crashes. Only po receives it.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/harness/decisoes_do_projeto.ex:34 (anexar), :49 (texto), apps/engine/lib/engine/session_events/event.ex:224 (list_types_for_project), apps/engine/lib/engine/sessions/engine_api_client.ex:729 (DecisoesDoProjeto)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/harness/decisoes_do_projeto_test.exs ("o turno do PO leva a decisão e só a pergunta AINDA aberta", "custo de entrada tem teto"; failure: "sem decisões nem perguntas não acrescenta nada", "leitura que falha deixa o turno sem a mensagem, sem queda")

    RN-876 — A needs_user_decision refused by the api goes back to QA, and only acceptance parks the gate

    • Rule: the QA gate only PARKS (gate.rescue_parked) when the api accepts the needs_user_decision verdict with nextAction: "await_user". If the api REFUSES (400, 404, or the 409 pergunta_ja_respondida), the refusal goes back to the Automation QA's loop as a new message (RN-163: an error is loop input), resuming the SAME context in which it emitted — on the 409, with the decision already taken for QA to apply instead of asking again. The other delegations do not run again. The return counts toward its own loop's iteration ceiling; once exhausted, the task is blocked with the reason and origin modelo (RN-059), never silently parked. Before, the api's return was discarded and the gate parked without a question in the thread (measured: openQuestionId: "1", a 400 from @IsUUID). The emit_qa_verdict tool refuses, INSIDE the loop and before the api, an openQuestionId that is not a uuid.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/qa_lead_server.ex:601 (devolver_ao_qa), apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/qa_lead_server.ex:312 (devolver_ao_qa), apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/qa_lead_server.ex:697 (texto_da_recusa), apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/qa_automacao_agent.ex:186 (devolver), apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/tools/emit_qa_verdict.ex:75 (uuid)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/gates/qa_lead_server_test.exs ("400: nada estaciona, o QA recebe o motivo e uma nova volta acontece"; "409 no laço: a volta seguinte do QA lê a decisão"; failure: "teto esgotado depois da recusa: bloqueio narrado, nunca estacionamento"), apps/engine/test/engine/harness/perguntas_em_aberto_test.exs ("AT-526 (RN-876): openQuestionId que não é uuid é recusa nomeada; uuid passa")
    • Origin: AT-526

    RN-877 — The QA cut says to check the decisions before asking for a decision

    • Rule: the QA cut's instruction says that, before needs_user_decision, it checks the listed decisions; a requirement that one of them already answers is not a question and the decision applies. openQuestionId only with the uuid of an OPEN question; without it, the new question goes in pergunta/sugestao. It is a reading reminder: it does not change the verdict yardstick.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/recorte_da_tarefa.ex:20 (texto)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/gates/recorte_da_tarefa_test.exs ("AT-526 (RN-877): manda conferir as decisões antes de needs_user_decision"; the instruction's failure is covered by the RN-876 test of the tool)
    • Origin: AT-526

    RN-878 — The execution agent's language applies from the first sentence

    • Rule: in the execution agents' turns WITHOUT a human author (dev-*, qa-*), RN-622's language guidance says it applies from the FIRST sentence, including the narration before each tool, and loses the caveat "unless the user asks" (there is no user in the turn). Measured in session 6c68fbc0 (pt-BR project, anthropic/claude-haiku-5.5): the guidance arrived, and even so iteration 0 of qa-automacao and of dev-encurtador-api opened in English ("I'll start by running…"). The path is still ONE (the facade, RN-622): nothing in a server prompt, agent_instructions or tool description (RN-667). The 50-token ceiling (AT-169) holds: measured with gpt-tokenizer, pt-BR 32/26, en 19/19 and the generic one with a short-form code in the worst case 30/30 (cl100k/o200k); a code outside the short form falls back to the usual guidance, which already fit. A turn with an author and the conversational agents do not change. Without a resolved language, nothing is added. Effectiveness is measured in the next TP-01.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/harness/idioma_da_resposta.ex:164 (orientacao_de_execucao), :154 (agente_de_execucao?)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/harness/idioma_da_resposta_test.exs ("agente de execução: vale desde a primeira frase, dentro do teto"; failure: "agente de execução sem idioma resolvido: nada é acrescentado")
    • Origin: AT-530

    RN-879 — The deliverable's absolute URL with the port, and the same code for the same error

    • Rule: when the task touches the entry point or routes, the dev's clause says that an absolute URL returned by the deliverable's API (e.g. the short link) comes from PUBLIC_BASE_URL or from the request's Host WITH the port — never a fixed http://localhost nor the hostname without a port —, and that the same error case responds with the same code on all routes. The QA cut checks both things and treats the divergence (e.g. not_found × link_not_found) as an ITEM. Owner decision of 10/14: a general rule.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/dev/dev_agent_server.ex:683 (clausula_de_openapi); apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/recorte_da_tarefa.ex:122 (secao_do_ponto_de_entrada)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/dev/report_done_instrucao_test.exs ("RN-879/880: tarefa que toca rotas leva URL com porta, code único e CORS" and the failure "RN-879/880 falha: tarefa sem rota não recebe CORS nem PUBLIC_BASE_URL"); apps/engine/test/engine/gates/recorte_da_tarefa_test.exs ("RN-879/880: URL, code de erro e CORS")
    • Origin: AT-531

    RN-880 — The deliverable answers configurable CORS, with relative servers and a pinned swagger-ui

    • Rule: in the same clause, the deliverable's API answers CORS with origins from CORS_ORIGINS (default http://localhost:<porta> and http://127.0.0.1:<porta>), OPTIONS 204 and Access-Control-Allow-* with Authorization and Content-Type; /openapi.json declares servers only relative (/) — an absolute URL would create the CORS error when the page is opened through another host —, and swagger-ui from a CDN goes with an exact version, never @5. The QA proves it with a preflight from another origin (curl -X OPTIONS) and fails it without the headers, with absolute servers or with the floating CDN.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/dev/dev_agent_server.ex:683 (clausula_de_openapi); apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/recorte_da_tarefa.ex:122 (secao_do_ponto_de_entrada)
    • Test: the same as RN-879 (happy path and failure in both files)
    • Origin: AT-532

    RN-881 — The engine prints the project's other decisions for the dev and QA

    • Rule: the decisoesDoProjeto the api returns in dev-context (RN-872, ADR 0215, already with a ceiling) becomes the section "Outras decisões do projeto", separate and AFTER the task's decisions, in the dev's story unit and in the QA cut. Empty or absent writes nothing; the engine applies no ceiling of its own.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/dev/context_builder.ex:133 (secao_de_decisoes_do_projeto); apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/recorte_da_tarefa.ex:20 (texto)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/dev/context_builder_test.exs ("RN-881: decisoesDoProjeto vira a seção..." and "RN-881 falha: sem decisoesDoProjeto a seção some"); apps/engine/test/engine/gates/recorte_da_tarefa_test.exs ("RN-881: outras decisões do projeto")
    • Origin: AT-526 (slice F4, engine side)

    RN-885 — The deliverable's CORS uses the PUBLISHED port, and QA checks the header on both origins

    • Rule: revises RN-880 (which still holds for the rest). The default of the deliverable's CORS_ORIGINS uses the PUBLISHED port — APP_PORT, which the compose passes to the app —, never the internal PORT: in TP-01 (16th) the default came out with PORT=3000 and, with the compose publishing 8080, no real origin received Access-Control-Allow-Origin (measured with curl: :3000 receives, :8080 does not; in Chrome, Failed to fetch). QA runs the preflight against the PUBLISHED port, once for http://localhost:<APP_PORT> and again for http://127.0.0.1:<APP_PORT>, and fails it without the Access-Control-Allow-Origin header — a 204 alone is not enough, and CORS only on the internal port is an ITEM.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/dev/dev_agent_server.ex:683 (clausula_de_openapi); apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/recorte_da_tarefa.ex:122 (secao_do_ponto_de_entrada)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/dev/report_done_instrucao_test.exs ("RN-885: o padrão do CORS usa a porta PUBLICADA, nunca a interna"; failure: "RN-879/880 falha: tarefa sem rota não recebe CORS nem PUBLIC_BASE_URL"); apps/engine/test/engine/gates/recorte_da_tarefa_test.exs ("RN-885: o preflight é na porta PUBLICADA, nas duas origens, e confere o header")
    • Origin: AT-535

    RN-890 — QA checks the exit code of the command-line command, on refusal too

    • Rule: the Automation QA's instruction orders checking, in the test that proves a command-line command of the deliverable (CLI, package.json script), exit code 0 on the happy path and non-zero when the command refuses the input; a test without that check on refusal is a rule with no coverage and goes into itens. The QA-strategy instruction asks, in criteriosExecutaveis, for these two criteria for every command that refuses input. It is an agent instruction: no new gate, no change of verdict or structure.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/qa_automacao_agent.ex:250 (instrucao_de_codigo_de_saida), apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/qa_estrategia_agent.ex:129 (instrucao_de_codigo_de_saida)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/gates/qa_automacao_agent_test.exs ("a instrução cobra código de saída 0 no feliz e diferente de 0 na recusa"); apps/engine/test/engine/gates/qa_estrategia_agent_test.exs ("os arquivos da entrega entram na mensagem")
    • Origin: AT-542 (TP-01, 17th: npm run user:create refused the password and exited with 0)

    RN-893 — The PO declares the edge of the provided value in the RF and the DoD

    • Rule: the PO's kickoff instruction (with or without an event log) carries a third obligation: every RF that receives a value provided by the user (date, number, text, list, limit) declares, in the RF and the DoD, whether the edge — past/future, zero, empty, negative, the limit itself — is ACCEPTED or REFUSED (with the HTTP status/error); the example is an expiration in the past. When unsure, the PO calls declare_open_question with afeta.historias and a suggestion, never inventing or omitting. A PROMPT rule, in the mold of RN-790: no tool refuses a story without an edge. The PO does NOT start rewriting business_rule; Creative, QA, schema and gate do not change.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/agents/po_server.ex:692 (obrigacoes)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/agents/po_server_test.exs:438 (the kickoff instruction carries the edge obligation) and :460 (without an event log, the kickoff still carries it)
    • Origin: AT-541 (TP-01 of 10/14: the deliverable accepted expiresAt in the past and the link was born expired, 410; the story's RF only said the expiration is optional), owner's decision of 10/15

    RN-895 — The argument refusal says what arrived and the expected format

    • Rule: revises RN-719 and RN-725. A field declared array/object that arrives as non-JSON text, or as a number/boolean/wrong type, is refused before the tool with "the field X arrived as ; send ", with an example (["item 1","item 2"], {"chave":"valor"}), never with the value. The emit_plano_de_teste sentence now puts the keys in backticks, so the refusal carries the received keys.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/harness/argumentos_de_ferramenta.ex:84 (coagir); apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/tools/emit_plano_de_teste.ex:73 (run)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/harness/argumentos_de_ferramenta_test.exs:193 and :201 (the format and the received type) and :210 (the received keys)
    • Origin: AT-583 (TP-01 18th and 19th: dod arrived as a markdown checklist and the test plan was refused 4 times without saying what came)

    RN-896 — The epic_id is validated before the api

    • Rule: create_story refuses an epic_id that is not a UUID ("epic_id inválido; …"). When the PO already created epics in this process (epicos_conhecidos) and the id is not among them, it rereads the backlog ONCE (list_backlog): if the id is there, it accepts; otherwise it refuses listing the backlog's id=título (cap of 20 lines, stating the total if truncated, ADR 0060). An empty map or a failing backlog read means "unknown" and does not refuse — the api decides.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/harness/tools/create_story.ex:57 (epico_valido)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/harness/argumentos_de_ferramenta_test.exs:224 and :233
    • Origin: AT-584 (qwen sent EPIC_ID_PLACEHOLDER_NAO_USAR_AQUI and the api answered with the DTO name)

    RN-897 — A provider cut by max_tokens in a gate is origin modelo

    • Rule: the agent.error of the qa-estrategia, qa-automacao, qa-performance-seguranca and appsec gates classifies "Tool calls cutoff by max_tokens" as modelo (the reasoning ate the cap, RN-059); any other LLM error stays infra.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/origem_do_erro.ex:10 (de_llm)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/gates/origem_do_erro_test.exs:7
    • Origin: AT-586 (BaseTen 502 recorded as infra)

    RN-898 — A response cut off by the ceiling, with no text at all, closes the turn out loud

    • Rule: the turn of a conversational agent whose last LLM response was cut off by the output ceiling (RN-737) and that wrote no text around it does NOT close silently: the line "Response cut off by the length limit." (pt-BR: "Resposta cortada pelo limite de tamanho.") becomes the closing of an agent.response with modelName, and the bubble shows "Continue where it stopped" (RN-749). It holds for the seven conversational agents, which write the closing through payload_do_turno/2. It violates RN-059 and RN-163 when missing.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/agents/texto_do_turno.ex:65 (payload_do_turno)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/harness/resposta_cortada_test.exs:104
    • Origin: AT-570 (TP-01 18th: the PO's 3rd call came out 8192/8192 and the turn closed in idle with no agent.response)

    RN-899 — An empty response with no cut-off becomes an agent.error of origin model

    • Rule: the close of the conversational loop with no text, no tool and no cut-off writes an agent.error with origin modelo and the phrase "o modelo não devolveu resposta" (FalhaDeTurno), in the seven servers. It is the agent's speech to the heartbeat (RN-581): a session with a silent turn closed by heartbeat_timeout 26 s after the user left the screen. Per-tab presence of the project does NOT count (RN-064).
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/agents/falha_de_turno.ex:63 (origem), apps/engine/lib/engine/agents/po_server.ex:327 (gravar_texto_do_turno)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/agents/po_server_test.exs:194 and apps/engine/test/engine/agents/texto_do_turno_test.exs:96
    • Origin: AT-572 (TP-01 18th, finding 4)

    RN-900 — write_file refuses to rewrite the README and drop the Infra's usage section

    • Rule: write_file replaces the whole file. When the path is a README (README*), the current file has the ## Rodar na sua máquina section and the new content does not, the tool refuses with a named error and writes nothing. Changing a fragment requires rewriting the full README, keeping the section. The title is one constant (titulo_secao_de_uso/0), also used by the Infra and dev prompts, so the refusal and the instruction do not diverge. It is not a gate: the refusal belongs to the tool itself and does not cover writes by terminal (cat >, sed -i).
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/harness/tools/write_file.ex:62 (recusa_de_secao_perdida)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/harness/tools/write_file_test.exs:41 (a rewrite without the section is refused and the file stays intact); the happy path is covered by the first two tests in the same file
    • Origin: AT-571 (TP-01 18th run of 10/19: the dev agent rewrote the README while fixing a QA finding and the usage section the Infra had written was lost; no gate noticed)

    RN-903 — The provider's 429 is the model's usage limit, not a network failure

    • Rule: a task the dev agent blocks because the model answered 429 (code rate_limit, or text with (429) or "limite de uso") says in the reason "limite de uso do modelo atingido (429)", and the automatic retry says that, if it repeats, the dev agent's model should be changed. It never says "falha passageira de rede" (transient network failure). The origin stays infra and the RN-864 retry does not change (up to 3, after 1, 3 and 10 min; a 60 s pause, which dev.idle names as "depois de limite de uso do modelo"). A network failure keeps its usual text.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/dev/dev_agent_server.ex:1202 (limite_de_uso?), apps/engine/lib/engine/dev/dev_agent_server.ex:1089 (motivo_da_parada), apps/engine/lib/engine/dev/dev_agent_server.ex:1062 (bloquear_passageira), apps/engine/lib/engine/dev/agent_io.ex:293 (pausar_por_rede)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/dev/dev_agent_server_test.exs ("429 do provider: o motivo diz limite de uso do modelo, não rede"; failure: "falha de rede continua com o texto de rede, não de limite")
    • Origin: AT-594 (TP-01 20th, 10/19: the 429 of laguna-s-2.1:free blocked the tasks as "falha passageira de rede", while the diagnosis said usage limit)

    RN-904 — The output-ceiling cut-off does not repeat the call with the whole catalog

    • Rule: when Jev restricts the menu and the model answers WITHOUT a tool, the step is repeated ONCE with the whole catalog (RN-625) — except when the response came CUT OFF by the output ceiling (truncated). The cut-off is not a menu error: going back with the whole catalog would only enlarge the prompt and spend another output ceiling. It holds in both facades (llm_turn and llm_turn_stream, the latter only with no text already delivered). Whoever handles the cut-off is RN-780's retry in the ToolLoop, which does not change.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/harness/roteamento_de_ferramenta.ex:126 (repetir_com_catalogo_inteiro?)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/harness/roteamento_de_ferramenta_test.exs:274 (a cut-off empty response does not repeat, on both paths; without a cut-off, it repeats)
    • Origin: AT-578 (EP-026, survey of 21/10): the PO's 1st turn in the 18th round spent two round trips of 8192 output tokens.

    RN-905 — The step ceiling says what was recorded and what is missing, and offers to continue

    • Rule: when a conversational agent stops at the step limit, the ceiling event (toolloop.limit_reached; for the Creative, the agent.error with reason: "limite_de_iteracoes") carries gravado, the RN-731 line read BEFORE the turn text is written (it goes there and not in the agent.response, never in both). The PO also carries regrasSemHistoria, the uncoveredCount that fechar_handoff already reads; a failed read leaves the key absent, never an error. The thread shows the reason without jargon, the recorded line, the rules without a story and the "Continue where you left off" button (RN-749, a user message that the RN-673 queue absorbs while a turn runs) only for the seven conversational agents with the session active; dev-*/qa-* get the text, with no button. The button never continues on its own.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/agents/po_server.ex:254 (run_turn); apps/web/src/routes/session-timeline-montagem.tsx:1098 (conversacional)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/agents/po_server_test.exs:548 (gravado and regrasSemHistoria; without the read, no key); apps/web/src/routes/session-timeline-montagem.falha-de-turno.test.tsx:108 (bubble, button to the same agent; no button for dev-* and inactive session)
    • Origin: AT-590 (EP-031).

    RN-908 — The PO's kickoff asks for the backlog in a batch, after reading, in layers

    • Rule: the PO's kickoff orders, in this order: read listar_regras_de_negocio and listar_backlog in the same response, with no write alongside; then all the epics in a single response; then all the stories, with the returned epic_id; then all the tasks, with the returned story_id. It never recreates an epic that already exists, and at most about 10 tool calls per response. Measured reason: without the batch request, nemotron makes 1 tool call per response and qwen makes 3; with it, 9 each (parallel_tool_calls changed nothing). Reading first is what stops qwen from creating epics without reading the backlog. It does not change the @max_iterations ceiling (RN-166).
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/agents/po_server.ex:617 (build_kickoff)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/agents/po_server_test.exs:518 (reading before writing; layered batch epics → stories → tasks)
    • Origin: AT-591 probe (21/10), implemented in AT-596 (EP-026).

    RN-913 — A repeated identical tool failure in the turn corrects on the 2nd and stops on the 3rd

    • Rule: in each turn, the harness counts IDENTICAL tool failures (same tool and same arguments, tool.result ok: false). On the 2nd, the result returned to the model gains the correction "this call has already failed the same way… change the arguments or use another approach". On the 3rd, the loop STOPS and writes an agent.error with origin modelo (reason: falha_repetida_de_ferramenta), never silently (RN-059, RN-163). Different failures do not add up, and the counter resets every turn. It holds for the seven conversational agents and for the ToolLoop, through the single dispatch of RN-719; each loop stops with the outcome it already had (the conversational ones through the iteration-ceiling path, with the agent.error in place of toolloop.limit_reached; the ToolLoop returning {:ok, ctx} with last_error).
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/harness/argumentos_de_ferramenta.ex:26 (executar), apps/engine/lib/engine/harness/falha_repetida.ex:30 (registrar), :51 (parou?), :56 (narrar), apps/engine/lib/engine/harness/tool_loop.ex:123 (parou_ou_loop)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/harness/falha_repetida_test.exs (2nd corrects, 3rd stops in the ToolLoop, different failures do not add up, resets between turns)
    • Origin: AT-588 (EP-026), the owner's decision of 22/10 (K=2 corrects, K=3 stops).

    RN-918 — The agent writes its own plan with registrar_plano, and the last agent.plan is the current one

    • Rule: registrar_plano writes agent.plan with the goal (up to 200 characters) and from 1 to 12 steps of up to 160 characters, each one pendente, em_curso, feito or descartado. Each call sends the WHOLE plan and replaces the previous one (the current one is the actor's last agent.plan). Above the ceiling the tool REFUSES, naming the limit, and never cuts. It is not a proposed_action (writing the plan is not an external effect) nor an artifact (outside ferramentas_de_artefato/0) and the description does not name a language (RN-667). The six conversational agents with the Infra Lead, the dev and the QA and AppSec subagents have the tool; the Infra file-validation subagent and the context-manager are left out. Reidratacao shows only each actor's last plan, as a note, and skips the tool's tool.call/tool.result.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/harness/tools/registrar_plano.ex:84 (run), apps/engine/lib/engine/agents/reidratacao.ex:172 (so_o_ultimo_plano_de_cada_ator)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/agents/diario_vivo_test.exs (registrar_plano: writes, replaces, refuses above the ceiling; Reidratacao: only the last plan)
    • Origin: AT-582 (EP-031), the owner's decision of 27/10; ADR 0226.

    RN-919 — The journal is the projection of the log plus the plan, with no table, and says what is confirmed and what was left without an outcome

    • Rule: Engine.Agents.Diario reads the tail of the agent's log, from the turn marker (agent.status: working, the task's dev.working or the gate subagent's agent.turn_resumed), and returns the current plan, the confirmed effects (tool.result ok, proposed_action.executed, artifacts, preserved commit), the calls without an outcome, the pending actions (proposed_action.created without a decision), the last activity and whether the automatic resumption has already been used. The source of truth remains the event log. RetomadaDoTurno turns the journal into an EPHEMERAL system message (ceiling of 2,000 characters, after DecisoesDoProjeto and before PerfilDoAutor; in the ToolLoop, through ctx[:retomada]): where it stopped, the plan, what not to repeat, what to check first and the pending actions that are not proposed again. It is never a fake user message. Push and PR are repeatable (the open PR is reused, RN-812); merge NEVER on resumption (RN-418); the engine does not re-execute a call without an outcome, the model decides, after checking the state.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/agents/diario.ex:85 (de_eventos), :255 (retomada_usada?), apps/engine/lib/engine/harness/retomada_do_turno.ex:69 (texto)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/agents/diario_vivo_test.exs (Diario.de_eventos/2, RetomadaDoTurno, marker by option and used resumption)
    • Origin: AT-582 (EP-031); ADR 0226.

    RN-920 — The conversational turn killed by the restart is resumed ONCE at boot, if recent

    • Rule: revises RN-586. The boot sweep (TurnoOrfao.varrer/2) resumes the orphan turn whose agent had activity at most 900 s ago (:retomada_automatica_janela_segundos, the same ruler as RN-823, measured by the agent's last activity and not by the working) and that has not yet been resumed: it writes agent.error (turno_interrompido_por_reinicio, retomada: "automatica"), agent.status: idle and agent.turn_resumed (automatica: true) and wakes the agent, whose init/1 schedules :retomar_turno_interrompido BEFORE the queue delivery (RN-673). The ceiling is durable in the log: an agent.turn_resumed of the agent between the last non-working status and the orphan working. An old or already-resumed turn is only closed, with the phrase pointing to the button (RN-921). The fechar_ao_subir/3 of init/1 still only closes, and a mute node counts as alive (when in doubt it neither closes nor resumes). Declared residue: two nodes starting together, without seeing each other, may resume the same turn twice.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/agents/turno_orfao.ex:90 (varrer), :181 (retomar), apps/engine/lib/engine/agents/turno_assincrono.ex:430 (retomada_ao_subir), :253 (tratar_resultado)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/agents/diario_vivo_test.exs (TurnoOrfao.varrer/2: retomada automática 1× no boot, TurnoAssincrono: retomada no init); apps/engine/test/engine/agents/reidratacao_dos_seis_test.exs
    • Origin: AT-582 (EP-031), the owner's decision of 27/10; ADR 0226.

    RN-922 — A dev agent that crashes within the window resumes from the commit of the SAME task, once

    • Rule: revises RN-742 (and cites RN-778, which still only closes the dev with no durable row). The rehydrated working dev, with its last activity at most 900 s ago and no dev.working cause: "retomada" on this task, preserves the worktree as a commit (RN-743), re-adopts the SAME task (its branch, RN-779), writes dev.working with cause: "retomada" and the commit, and runs the loop with the journal's message. The task never leaves in_progress. The 2nd crash, a crash outside the window, awaiting_approval and any failure of the resumption block as before, and the manual "unblock" becomes the "resume from the commit". Merge never happens here (RN-418). RN-864's transient-failure counter is another ruler and does not change.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/dev/dev_agent_server.ex:1290 (retomada_do_commit), :1316 (retomar_do_commit)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/dev/retomada_do_commit_test.exs (a recent crash resumes; a 2nd crash, outside the window and awaiting_approval block)
    • Origin: AT-582 (EP-031), the owner's decision of 27/10; ADR 0226.

    RN-923 — The automatic gate rescue holds once per cycle, at boot and on the tick, and "Resume gate" resets the counter

    • Rule: revises RN-823 and RN-722. engine.gate_states.auto_resumes counts the cycle's automatic re-dispatches (at boot and on the tick); with 1 already used, the second rescue PARKS (gate.rescue_parked, motivo: "retomada_automatica_ja_usada") and waits for "Resume gate" (RN-724), which has no ceiling and RESETS the counter. The counter stays outside the upsert's on_conflict (it survives the cycle's steps) and disappears with the row when the cycle ends. The 2 h parking does not change. The gate subagents gain registrar_plano; on re-dispatch the ToolLoop receives ctx[:retomada] with the plan from the subagent's last agent.turn_resumed, and the area is still re-dispatched whole. Declared residue: a new cycle may pick up a pending marker younger than 15 min, and the gate subagent does not open a working, so its resumption carries only the plan.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/gate_rescuer.ex:212 (retomar_ou_estacionar), :359 (retomar_estacionado), apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/gate_state.ex:37 (auto_resumes)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/gates/gate_rescuer_test.exs:501 (teto de 1 retomada automática on the tick and at boot, and the manual one resetting)
    • Origin: AT-582 (EP-031), the owner's decisions of 27 and 28/10; ADR 0226.

    RN-924 — The gate correction that reaches the Infra Lead with a turn in progress is durable

    • Rule: revises RN-617, on the pattern of the queue in RN-673. InfraLeadServer.correct/3 writes infra.correction_queued (id, gate, reason, diagnosis up to 2,000 characters) BEFORE the cast and, with no process, wakes the agent; iniciar_correcao/2 writes infra.correction_delivered (id). The init/1 rebuilds the pending ones (queued minus delivered) and delivers them after the resumed turn and the message queue, and the Rehydrator wakes the Infra Lead that has a pending correction at boot. A delivered one is not delivered again. The Infra Lead also has registrar_plano and the resumption of RN-920.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/infra/infra_lead_server.ex:1649 (correct), :408 (iniciar_correcao), :200 (init)
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/infra/infra_lead_server_test.exs:1980 (correct/3 grava infra.correction_queued antes do cast; the pending one survives the restart and the delivered one is not delivered again)
    • Origin: AT-582 (EP-031); ADR 0226.

    RN-940 — The QA-automation gate brings up an ephemeral QA instance, tests on it and removes it

    • Rule: in container/mounted, before the loop the QaAutomacaoAgent proposes qa_environment_start (seeded auto_approve); a start-up that fails ends the gate with a named outcome of origin infra (RN-059). The QA's terminal carries alvo: {qa: true, taskId} coming from the agent's ctx, never from the model, and runs on the instance; the other agents do not change. The instance is removed on success, failure, exception and exhausted ceiling, except for a loop stopped waiting for a decision. runner still has no instance. The GateRescuer's boot and tick call the sweep with the tasks of non-parked qa gate_states; a failure only logs. run.qa is validated in the engine with the same ruler as the api (RN-935).
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/qa_automacao_agent.ex:78 (subir_instancia), apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/qa_automacao_agent.ex:120 (com_remocao), apps/engine/lib/engine/harness/hooks/action_pipeline.ex:46 (maybe_put_alvo), apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/gate_rescuer.ex:102 (varrer_instancias_de_qa), apps/engine/lib/engine/infra/instrucoes_de_execucao.ex:133 (qa).
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/gates/qa_automacao_agent_instancia_test.exs, apps/engine/test/engine/infra/instrucoes_de_execucao_qa_test.exs.
    • Origin: AT-612 (EP-030), ADR 0229; depends on the api (#1146).

    RN-944 — The automation QA receives the previous round's request, the plan is read by type and the dev knows where write_file writes

    • Rule: (a) the most recent changes_requested from actor qa for the SAME task goes into the dev_context as :pedido_anterior, and the QaAutomacaoAgent adds to the message the list of items and the instruction to say in the resumo whether each one was addressed; with no previous request (first round, approved, another task, empty list) the message is the usual one. It is input, never a new verdict ruler (pattern of ADR 0192): the correction ceiling (RN-015) and the single qa_verdict per cycle do not change. (b) plano_ja_emitido/3 reads by type (Reidratacao.eventos_do_tipo/3), not the window of the 200 most recent: the task's plan does not leave the window nor is it paid for again (fulfils RN-674). (c) The dev-*/qa-* environment says that write_file/read_file use a path relative to the repository root and that cd in the terminal does not persist (each call is a new sh -c in System.cmd ... cd: dir, terminal_executor.ex).
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/qa_lead_server.ex:784 (pedido_anterior), apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/qa_lead_server.ex:743 (plano_ja_emitido), apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/qa_automacao_agent.ex:323 (com_o_pedido_anterior), apps/engine/lib/engine/harness/ambiente_do_agente.ex:102 (montar).
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/gates/qa_automacao_agent_test.exs, apps/engine/test/engine/gates/qa_lead_server_test.exs, apps/engine/test/engine/harness/ambiente_do_agente_test.exs.
    • Origin: AT-616 (EP-030), TP-01 (21st) of 30/10/2026.

    RN-945 — The QA agents name the output-ceiling cut-off instead of "the model stopped"

    • Rule: extends RN-780 to the two QA agents: when the ToolLoop returns corte_pelo_teto: true, QaEstrategiaAgent and QaAutomacaoAgent narrate, with origin modelo, that the response was only reasoning and the output ceiling cut it off twice (single text in Engine.Gates.MotivoDoCorte). Without the marker, the old diagnosis ("parou sem chamar ...") stands. A plan failure still does not hold back the review (RN-674) and the iteration ceiling does not change.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/qa_estrategia_agent.ex:265 (diagnostico_de_parada), apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/qa_automacao_agent.ex:427 (diagnostico_de_parada), apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/motivo_do_corte.ex:8 (texto).
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/gates/qa_estrategia_agent_test.exs, apps/engine/test/engine/gates/qa_automacao_agent_test.exs.
    • Origin: AT-618 part 1 (EP-030), TP-01 (21st) of 30/10/2026.

    RN-946 — The cut-off by the ceiling in the middle of the text is also named by the QA agents

    • Rule: extends RN-945/RN-780: a truncated response WITH text and no call (the reasoning ate the ceiling and the text came out cut off) is not "the model stopped": the ToolLoop returns corte_com_texto: true, a key of its OWN (not corte_pelo_teto, which the dev agent and RN-780 match), and QaEstrategiaAgent and QaAutomacaoAgent narrate, with origin modelo, that the ceiling cut the response in the middle of the text before the finishing tool (MotivoDoCorte.texto_com_texto). There is no new retry (it would change loop and cost); a non-cut response with text stays "parou sem chamar ...". The same gap in the dev agent is left out.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/harness/tool_loop.ex:256 (parada_sem_chamada), apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/qa_estrategia_agent.ex:265 (diagnostico_de_parada), apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/qa_automacao_agent.ex:427 (diagnostico_de_parada), apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/motivo_do_corte.ex:9 (texto_com_texto).
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/harness/tool_loop_test.exs, apps/engine/test/engine/gates/qa_estrategia_agent_test.exs, apps/engine/test/engine/gates/qa_automacao_agent_test.exs.
    • Origin: AT-620 (EP-030), TP-01 (22nd) of 31/10/2026.

    RN-955 — An internal GET with a poisoned pool connection is repeated ONCE

    • Rule: complements RN-742 (AT-428). The late response of a request that blew the receive_timeout stays in the socket of a Finch pool connection; the next GET that picks it up reads the OLD response and throws CaseClauseError {:status, ref, _}, which the pending-work check of RN-064 read as "api down" and ended the active session. req_get repeats the SAME GET once when the error is that one (Finch has already discarded the connection); only GET, because a POST may have had an effect. A true transport error is NOT repeated, and RN-064 does not change.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/sessions/engine_api_client.ex:2200 (get_repetindo).
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/sessions/engine_api_client_isolado_test.exs (repeats and returns; a failure on the second has no third; a true timeout does not repeat).
    • Origin: AT-642 (EP-030).

    RN-956 — readiness and offer-infra-handoff bring the agent up before calling, like message

    • Rule: after closing and reopening (RN-649) the conversational agents are not up, and the two routes called the server without start_agent: the :noproc became a 500 masked as "Stale conn". They now receive projectId and bring the Creative/Architect up first (idempotent); without projectId, a named 400.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine_web/controllers/agent_command_controller.ex:348 (readiness), :355 (offer_infra_handoff).
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine_web/controllers/agent_command_controller_test.exs (a stopped agent comes up; without projectId it is 400).
    • Origin: AT-642 (EP-030).

    RN-957 — "aborted" is an infra-origin failure

    • Rule: complements RN-059 and RN-810. "aborted" is the provider response's socket dying in the middle of the stream; the final frame without errorCode fell into codigo. FalhaDeTurno.origem/1 classifies it infra (without touching falha_de_rede?; the retry still goes through RN-864).
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/agents/falha_de_turno.ex:87 (origem), :110.
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/agents/falha_de_turno_test.exs ("RN-957").
    • Origin: AT-642 (EP-030).

    RN-962 — The deliverable's compose uses a named volume, never a relative bind-mount

    • Rule: the Infra Lead asks for a NAMED VOLUME for persistent data and for a .env.example with only the required variables, with no value. propose_infra_pr refuses, BEFORE opening the PR, a compose file with a relative bind-mount (- ./pasta:/destino or source: ./pasta): the folder is born root on the first start-up and the non-root app cannot write to it. The refusal is a tool result (it enters the loop, RN-163) and records a tool.result with ok: false.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/infra/infra_lead_server.ex:669 (recusa_de_bind_mount_relativo).
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/infra/infra_lead_server_test.exs (a named volume passes; the short, quoted and long forms are refused).
    • Origin: AT-644 (EP-031).

    RN-982 — The sources of an answer are the turn's own ok reads, recorded by the server, never by the model's text

    • Rule: sister of RN-731. The server notes each ok read of the turn (listings of rules, backlog, metrics, ADRs and contracts, read_file and the rag_search snippets) and the agent.response carries fontes OUTSIDE the content: {tipo, rotulo, ref?, searchId?, chunkId?}, ceiling of 10, with fontesTotal when it cuts (ADR 0060). With no read, the field does not exist. A read that failed is not a source. The screen says "consulted", never "used": it is what the agent read, not what it made use of. It does not change the content, the rehydration (RN-580), the tool.result (RN-589) or the RAG vote (RN-479..481).
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/agents/fontes_do_turno.ex:37 (anotar), apps/engine/lib/engine/agents/texto_do_turno.ex:65 (payload_do_turno), apps/engine/lib/engine/harness/tool_loop.ex:419 (anotar).
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/agents/fontes_do_turno_test.exs, apps/engine/test/engine/harness/tool_loop_test.exs ("RN-982"), apps/engine/test/engine/agents/retomada_dos_seis_test.exs and apps/engine/test/engine/infra/infra_lead_server_test.exs (the tool in all seven).
    • Conversational agents (slice 2): the SEVEN conversational agents offer the SAME rag_search as the dev agents (Engine.Harness.Tools.RagSearch, project scope, no scope parameter, the tool's top_k ceiling, ADR 0060), dispatched by ArgumentosDeFerramenta.executar/4 (RN-719) and with the result in the tool.result (RN-589). It enters the spec and the dispatch of each server: criativo_server.ex:121/:469, po_server.ex:153/:437, arquiteto_server.ex:125/:402, dev_lead_server.ex:187/:580, ux_designer_server.ex:121/:350, staff_server.ex:121/:304, infra/infra_lead_server.ex:239/:1149.
    • Origin: AT-716 slices 1 and 2 (EP-031).

    RN-985 — The inline citation is marked by the model and validated against the turn's own sources; an invented index is not shown

    • Rule: when the turn already has a source (RN-982), an EPHEMERAL system message at the end of the call (same path as RN-622, never in state.messages, naming no language, RN-667) lists the sources as [n] label (up to 10, in the order of fontes) and asks "cite com [n]; não cite o que não está na lista" (ceiling of 250 tokens; 10 sources measure ~55). At the close, the server extracts the [n] from the text and writes citacoes in the agent.response with only those that exist in fontes (1 <= n <= fontes.length), without repetition and in order. The content is NOT rewritten (RN-731): the invalid marker stays in the text and the screen discards it. With no sources, or no valid marker, the field does not exist.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/harness/citacao_das_fontes.ex:22 (anexar) and :43 (marcar), apps/engine/lib/engine/sessions/engine_api_client.ex:744 (llm_turn), apps/engine/lib/engine/agents/texto_do_turno.ex:65 (payload_do_turno), apps/engine/lib/engine/harness/tool_loop.ex:206 (marcar).
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/harness/citacao_das_fontes_test.exs, apps/engine/test/engine/harness/tool_loop_test.exs ("RN-985").
    • Origin: AT-716 slice 3 (EP-031).

    RN-990 — The attached image goes to the LLM only in the turn in which the message arrived

    • Rule: the anexos of the agent/message ([{id, nome, mime, tamanho}], up to 4 per message) enters the turn and the message queue (RN-673) TOGETHER with the text: chat.message_queued keeps the attachments, and the pending ones that become ONE turn join the attachments in order. On the turn's FIRST LLM call, the content of the last user message becomes the list of parts (text + one {type:'anexo',id} per attachment); the call CONSUMES the attachments, so the following iterations of the tool loop and the following turns send only the text with the label [anexos: a.png, b.txt] (ceiling of 300 characters), which is also what the rehydration returns from the chat.message's payload.anexos. The image is never resent (cost × iterations × turns; the owner's decision, 02/11). The path is a SINGLE one for the seven conversational agents (TurnoAssincrono + Reidratacao), with no per-server code; a message without attachments remains a string.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/harness/anexos_do_turno.ex:56 (anexar), apps/engine/lib/engine/agents/turno_assincrono.ex:324 (receber_mensagem), apps/engine/lib/engine/agents/reidratacao.ex:443 (usuario), apps/engine/lib/engine_web/controllers/agent_command_controller.ex:545 (autor_do_turno).
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/agents/anexos_do_turno_test.exs.
    • Origin: AT-715 slice 1, lane L3 (EP-031), ADR 0246.

    RN-996 — The QA verdict carries the per-test result read from the JUnit report by the engine; without a report, it says why

    • Rule: emit_qa_verdict accepts relatorioDeTestes (a relative path, usually .brabo-qa/junit.xml). The ENGINE reads the file (the model never declares it), deletes it and puts execucaoDeTestes in the subagent's qa_verdict and in the consolidated one: {fonte: "junit", total, passaram, falharam, pulados, duracaoMs, suites, casosOmitidos}, or {indisponivel: "sem_relatorio" | "relatorio_invalido" | "modo_runner", origem, detalhe?}. No report NEVER becomes "0 tests" (RN-470), and a read failure never changes the verdict (ADR 0054; it only records, the approval ruler is still exit 0 plus the matrix). Ceiling (ADR 0060): 200 cases written, failures first; 20 messages of 500 characters; names of 200; counters of the whole report, casosOmitidos by subtraction. The parser (:xmerl SAX) refuses DOCTYPE/ENTITY (XXE), reads at most 2 MiB and 20,000 cases. In runner the file comes through the runner channel (the already existing exec, with no new operation). Whoever installs the reporter is the dev agent, as a TASK that the Dev Lead includes in the module; the QA only reads.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/relatorio_junit.ex:34 (ler), apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/relatorio_junit.ex:111 (parse), apps/engine/lib/engine/gates/hooks/termination.ex:49 (com_execucao_de_testes).
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/gates/relatorio_junit_test.exs (vitest, pytest, XXE refused, size/case/write ceiling, missing/invalid/deleted file), apps/engine/test/engine/gates/hooks/termination_test.exs, apps/engine/test/engine/gates/qa_lead_test.exs.
    • Origin: AT-687 (EP-031), engine lane.

    RN-999 — The pending call's sibling call receives a refusal, so every tool_use has its tool_result

    Revises RN-284 and RN-593. They said the loop STOPS at the pending call "without processing the following calls". Stopping stands; what changed is that the following ones are no longer left mute.

    • Rule: when the model asks for several tools in the same response and one of them is left pending approval ({:pending, …} in the Dev Lead, aguardando_aprovacao in the ToolLoop of the dev agents and the QAs), each SIBLING call that came AFTER it does not run, but receives, before suspending, a tool message with "não executada: o turno parou esperando a aprovação de ; repita se ainda precisar" and a tool.result ok: false with the same text, through ResultadoDeFerramenta (RN-589). The pending one stays without a message until the outcome (RN-593).
    • Why: Anthropic refuses with a 400 the resumption that carries a tool_use without the corresponding tool_result (finding 1 of the TP-01 of 05/11: propose_execution_plan plus declare_open_question). The sibling is not executed, so the refusal says so and lets the model repeat it.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/agents/dev_lead_server.ex:582 (recusar_irmas), apps/engine/lib/engine/harness/tool_loop.ex:225 (recusar_irmas), apps/engine/lib/engine/agents/resultado_de_ferramenta.ex:44 (recusa_de_irma).
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/agents/dev_lead_server_test.exs ("irmã da pendente recebe recusa") and apps/engine/test/engine/harness/tool_loop_test.exs ("irmã da chamada pendente recebe recusa"); mutation proven: without the refusal both fail.
    • Origin: AT-739 (finding 1), Round 191, lane L1.

    RN-1003 — The agent closes an open question the user already answered in the chat, only when the answer is unambiguous

    Extends RN-862. Until now only the api closed an open question, through the button; the agent had no way to do it.

    • Rule: the four askers (Creative, PO, Architect, Dev Lead) have the tool answer_open_question (questionId, optional resposta; without it the agent's own suggestion stands). It calls POST /internal/projects/:projectId/open-questions/:questionId/answer with {sessionId, agentId, resposta?}. The api does the closing (the same core as the click: decision_record plus open_question.answered, re-arming the gate, RN-860), so the engine writes no event. already_answered comes back to the model as success ("already closed"); a 404 becomes a named refusal (the question is not in this project) and a 400 a refusal quoting the api. The central sentence in FluxoDoTime tells the model to close only when the user's chat answer is UNAMBIGUOUS, and to keep the question open otherwise. To make that possible, the open questions (with questionId and the suggestion) now reach the turn of all four askers, not only the PO's (DecisoesDoProjeto, same bounded local read, ADR 0060).
    • Why: TP-01 finding of 05/11: the user answered four questions in the chat, the Creative recorded the rules, and the questions stayed in the queue with "Accept suggestion". Decision of the owner (AT-741): the agent closes.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/harness/tools/answer_open_question.ex:43 (run), apps/engine/lib/engine/harness/fluxo_do_time.ex:109 (@frase_da_pergunta), apps/engine/lib/engine/harness/decisoes_do_projeto.ex:23 (@agentes).
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/harness/tools/answer_open_question_test.exs and apps/engine/test/engine/harness/decisoes_do_projeto_test.exs; mutation proven: breaking the 404 clause, already_answered, the agentId field, the unambiguous-answer sentence, the asker list or one agent's registration fails a test.
    • Origin: AT-741 (engine side), Round 193, lane L3; api side is RN-1002.

    RN-1006 — The Dev Lead creates the foundation task of a module that others depend on

    Revises RN-678 (the Dev Lead's role). ADR 0259.

    • Rule: the Dev Lead gets create_task (the PO's Engine.Harness.Tools.CreateTask, with an extra required module). It sends agentRole: "dev_lead" in the POST /internal/sessions/:id/tasks body so the session history credits the Dev Lead; the PO sends no such field and the api assumes po. It only runs for a story whose moduleIds contains module; otherwise the call is refused with a named message ("the story does not list the module in moduleIds") and nothing is created. module_map_vigente shows each module's dependsOn, and the kickoff tells the Dev Lead that a module another one depends on needs a FOUNDATION task (skeleton, package.json, entrypoint, exposed contract) and that, if nobody has it, it must create it with create_task BEFORE proposing the plan. The api refuses a plan that leaves such a module without a task ("X depends on Y, which has no task: create Y's foundation task", RN-1004); the refusal reaches the Dev Lead as any other propose_execution_plan error, so the loop goes on.
    • Why: TP-01 finding of 05/11: the plan left persistencia and api-http with no task and the dependent tasks blocked. The PO creates tasks before the module_map exists, so only the Dev Lead can see the gap. Decision of the owner (AT-742).
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/agents/dev_lead_tools.ex (spec_create_task, run_create_task, module_map_vigente), apps/engine/lib/engine/agents/dev_lead_server.ex (tool_specs, run_tool("create_task"), build_kickoff).
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/agents/dev_lead_tools_test.exs ("create_task do Dev Lead") and apps/engine/test/engine/agents/dev_lead_server_test.exs (kickoff dependsOn, create_task in the specs); mutation proven: dropping dependsOn, the spec or the module check fails a test.
    • Origin: AT-742 (engine side), Round 194, lane L2; api side is RN-1004 and RN-1005.

    RN-1010 — A conflict integrating dev at claim goes to the agent; only what it does not resolve blocks

    Revises RN-779 (the conflict used to abort and block) and mirrors RN-715 (merge conflict on the PR).

    • Rule: when the integration of the current dev into the task's worktree conflicts on files, the engine does NOT abort: the merge stays in progress with the markers in the files, and add_worktree returns {:error, {:conflito_de_integracao, worktree, files, reason}}. run_task then follows to the normal implement (it records dev.working with cause: "integration_conflict") with ONE extra pinned message between the task prompt and the worktree snapshot: resolve the listed files keeping what the previous task did and what dev brought, then git add and git commit --no-edit through terminal; do NOT run git merge or git merge --abort; only then implement the task. A conflict with no conflicted file keeps the old behaviour (abort, named string, block).
    • Guards: (1) on report_done, if MERGE_HEAD is still there or any of the conflicted files has a <<<<<<< / >>>>>>> line (======= is not checked: it is a Markdown title underline), the merge is aborted and the task blocks with the RN-779 reason ("conflito ao integrar a dev atual no worktree da task", origin codigo), the diagnosis saying the agent did not resolve it; report_blocked blocks as before. (2) preservar_em aborts a merge in progress BEFORE the WIP commit (RN-743), so a block in the middle of the conflict (ceiling, budget, report_blocked) never commits markers; the RN-715 path is protected too.
    • Why: TP-01 26th run (06/11): two tasks blocked in the same second asking the user to integrate dev by hand, and unblocking only repeated the cycle. The conflict happens BEFORE the task's work, so it is the task loop, not the correction loop; the gates still judge the branch at the end.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/dev/worktree_manager.ex (integrar_trabalho, conflito_nao_resolvido?, abortar_merge_em_curso, preservar_em), apps/engine/lib/engine/dev/dev_agent_server.ex (run_task, conflito_message, handle_outcome of report_done).
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/dev/worktree_manager_test.exs ("conflito deixa o merge em curso com os marcadores", "preservar_em com merge em curso aborta e não commita marcador") and apps/engine/test/engine/dev/conflito_de_integracao_test.exs (real git: the agent resolves and reaches awaiting_gate; unresolved blocks with no marker committed); mutation proven: restoring the abort, dropping the preservar_em guard, the run_task clause or the report_done guard each fails a test.
    • Origin: AT-747, Round 203, lane L2.

    RN-1011 — The Dev Lead's plan is refused when a task cites a route the contract declares in another module

    Complements RN-792 (story routes against the contract) and RN-678 (the task belongs to the module the Dev Lead assigns). ADR 0200.

    • Rule: propose_execution_plan reads the current module contract (list_module_contracts) and the backlog (list_backlog) after resolving the task ids and BEFORE propose_action. For each planned task it extracts the METHOD /path routes from the task title and description (RotasDoContrato.extrair/1); the owners of a route are the modules whose contract declares it. If the module assigned to the task is not among the owners, the call returns an error naming the task, the route and the owner module(s), and telling the Dev Lead to move the task to the owner or to split the domain part into a task of the domain module with create_task. A route absent from the contract is ignored (RN-792 covers it). A failure reading the contract or the backlog does not refuse: the plan goes on and the result carries a warning.
    • Why: TP-01 26th run (06/11): the Dev Lead assigned "Implement POST /horarios" to scheduling, but the v3 contract declares that route in api; the dev-scheduling agent blocked for a missing auth validator that only api consumes. The refusal comes before the proposal because in automatic mode the plan runs as soon as it is proposed.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/harness/rotas_do_contrato.ex (donos_divergentes/3), apps/engine/lib/engine/agents/dev_lead_tools.ex (conferir_donos).
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/harness/rotas_do_contrato_test.exs and apps/engine/test/engine/agents/dev_lead_tools_test.exs ("tarefa que cita rota de OUTRO módulo"); mutation proven: comparing with any module, ignoring the owners, or removing the check from run/2 fails a test.
    • Origin: AT-748 (lane a), Round 203, lane L5.

    RN-1012 — A task blocked by the model wakes the Dev Lead, who asks the Architect for the missing contract

    • Rule: when a dev agent ends a task with report_blocked (origin modelo), the engine delivers {:tarefa_bloqueada, %{task_id, modulo, reason, diagnosis}} to the Dev Lead through Engine.Dev.Wake. Merge conflicts (codigo), exhausted gates (politica), credit/infra and the iteration ceiling do NOT wake it. The Dev Lead turns the notice into a system message: with the Dev Lead idle a turn starts; with a turn in progress, a pending plan approval or a non-empty queue, the notice goes to the message queue (capped by the RN-673 ceiling) and is read when the turn ends. The notice tells it to check the contract and the module map (read_backlog), ask the Architect with pedir_ao_arquiteto and create the task in the owning module with create_task, or tell the user what is missing.
    • pedir_ao_arquiteto: records the request as a durable chat.message event of actor dev-lead in the session (the same session as the Architect; Reidratacao reads chat.message from any actor) and delivers it to the Architect as a message prefixed [Pedido do Dev Lead]. This is an agent-to-agent channel that is not a handoff (a handoff to an active agent is refused with 409). If the Architect is busy the message joins its queue.
    • Contract published: declare_module_contracts, on a version accepted by the api, delivers {:contrato_publicado, version} to the Dev Lead, who then creates the task in the owning module and re-proposes the plan. A refusal by the api delivers nothing.
    • Limit (shortcut): the Wake delivers at most once (ADR 0045): with no Dev Lead process alive (engine restart, or architecture phase) the notice is lost, and the human path (the blocked-tasks list in the chat, RN-826) remains. Unblocking the stuck task after the dependency merges stays human (RN-826/RN-777).
    • Why: TP-01 26th run (06/11): the dev blocked, correctly, and nobody acted until the conductor asked the Architect and the Dev Lead by hand.
    • Where: apps/engine/lib/engine/dev/dev_agent_server.ex (avisar_dev_lead), apps/engine/lib/engine/agents/dev_lead_server.ex (handle_info of :tarefa_bloqueada and :contrato_publicado, avisar), apps/engine/lib/engine/harness/tools/pedir_ao_arquiteto.ex, apps/engine/lib/engine/harness/tools/declare_module_contracts.ex.
    • Test: apps/engine/test/engine/dev/dev_agent_server_test.exs, apps/engine/test/engine/agents/dev_lead_server_test.exs, apps/engine/test/engine/harness/tools/pedir_ao_arquiteto_test.exs, apps/engine/test/engine/harness/tools/declare_module_contracts_test.exs; mutation proven: removing the Wake.deliver in report_blocked, moving it into block_task/4 (all origins), always starting the turn, deleting the handle_info clause, removing the append_event of the tool and removing the Wake.deliver of the contract each fails a test.
    • Origin: AT-748 (b), Round 203, lane L6.
